### Page 2

世界経済：AI・防衛関連需要を受け景況感は好調。一方でインフレ圧力の広がりも確認
 9月のグローバル製造業PMIは53.0（8月52.3）と堅調。非製造業は54.5（8月53.6）と40か月ぶりの高水準
― 製造業はAI・防衛関連投資需要を受け生産、新規受注ともに上昇。非製造業も幅広い業種で業況が改善
― 一方、仕入・販売価格指数はともに上昇。エネルギーや輸送価格上昇によるインフレ圧力の広がりを示唆
 国・地域別では米国・ユーロ圏が製造業・非製造業ともに改善。とりわけ米国の好調が目立つ
― 米国ではAI関連支出の拡大に伴い機械・設備投資および生産が増加。非製造業においても情報通信業が突出して好調
― ユーロ圏でも、AI投資に支えられたIT関連サービスが特に力強く拡大。専門・商業サービスも改善
1
製造業の景況感（購買担当者景気指数） 非製造業の景況感（購買担当者景気指数）
（注） 日米欧：S&P Global、中：国家統計局。購買担当者景気指数：企業に対するアン
ケート調査結果から作成した指数。新規受注や生産高、雇用、出荷遅延などの指数

に一定のウエイトを掛けて算出。50が景気拡大・縮小の分岐点
（出所）S&P Global、中国国家統計局より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
（注） 日米欧：S&P Global、中：国家統計局。購買担当者景気指数（非製造業）：企業に対
するアンケート調査結果から作成した指数。50が景気拡大・縮小の分岐点

### Page 3

金融市場概況：投資家心理が悪化し、日米とも株安・金利低下
 AIインフラ投資への信用不安の高まりから、日米株価はハイテク株を中心に下落
― 米新興クラウドサービスの社債価格急落が株安の契機に。原油高に伴うインフレ懸念も下押し圧力に
― 投資家心理が悪化するなかで、安全資産とされる日米国債に資金が流入、前月までの金利上昇基調は一巡
 ドル円は、1ドル＝150円台後半で方向感の乏しい展開
― 日米金利とも低下し、日米金利差は概ね横ばい圏で推移
― 原油高が輸入増を通じて円安要因となる一方、日米株安に伴うリスクオフや仏情勢悪化に伴うユーロ安は円高要因に
2
（出所）LSEGより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

米国：インフレ率は高止まり、油価再加速で当面は物価高が続く懸念
 原油高や関税の影響から物価は加速
―9月のコアCPIは前月比＋0.3％（年率＋4.1％）と8月（前月比＋0.3％、年率＋3.5％）から伸びが拡大
― コアサービスでは住居費や輸送サービスなどが押し上げに寄与。コア財については、衣料品や玩具などが上昇。関税引
き上げに伴う価格転嫁が続いている模様
 足元で原油価格が再加速していることを踏まえれば、当面は物価の上振れリスクが残存する懸念
― シカゴ連銀が公表する週次の小売物価指標は9月後半から10月初めにかけて上昇しており、物価の高止まりを示唆
3
（出所） 米労働省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
項目別コアCPI 週次小売物価（シカゴ連銀）

（出所） シカゴ連銀より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

米国：足元の消費はガソリン高や関税に伴う物価上昇で足踏みも、年末商戦は堅調な見通し
 消費は足踏み。ガソリン価格高騰や関税に伴う物価高が家計の購買力を下押し
―9月の実質小売売上高は前月比▲0.5％と減少。ガソリン小売が減少したほか、自動車や衣料品も低調に推移
 物価高は逆風も、2026年の年末商戦は堅調予想
― 米マスターカードは、実質小売売上高（除くガソリン・自動車）は前年比＋2.7％と、前年（同＋2.1％）から伸びが拡大する
と予想。雇用環境の改善などを背景に幅広い所得層で安定した消費がみられると指摘
4
（注） ガソリンはCPIを用いて実質化。その他は、8、9月のみ各品目に対応するCPIを用

米国：雇用は減速も、AI関連需要が下支え
 9月の非農業部門雇用者数は前月差＋2.9万人と市場予想（同＋9.0万人）を下回る結果。7月・8月も下方修正
― 民間部門が前月差＋4.6万人（8月同＋8.9万人）と増勢が鈍化したほか、政府部門が同▲1.7万人と再びマイナス転化
― 民間部門では、旺盛なAI投資需要などを背景に、製造や建設、建築・エンジニアリングなどが増加基調を維持したもの
の、情報、金融、専門サービスなどの高賃金セクターが減少
― 時間当たり賃金の伸びは前月比＋0.1％（8月同＋0.3％）と鈍化。こちらも市場予想（同＋0.3％）を下回る結果
― 総じて冴えない結果ながら、3カ月平均では＋5万人程度を維持しており、雇用は減速しつつも失速には至らず

5
（出所） 米労働省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 7

米国：対人型業種の雇用に底堅さ。労働参加率上昇で失業率は小幅上昇
 対人接触度の高い業種では雇用に底堅さ、AIによる代替が進みにくい可能性
― ヘルスケアや建設、飲食サービスなど、対人接触機会の多い業種では雇用が増加する一方、経理・会計サービス、ITシ
ステム開発などでは低調
 失業率は4.2％と小幅悪化も引き続き低水準を維持
― 主に16-24歳の若年層およびプライムエイジ層の労働参加率の上昇が押し上げに寄与した模様
6
（注）1.バブルの大きさは各産業の雇用者数
2. 対人接触度は、O*NETの職業別のPhysical Proximity（業務上、他者と物理的に近
接する度合い）を、各産業の職業別雇用者構成で加重平均した指数
3.コロナ禍からの雇用回復の影響を除くため、雇用者数の変化率は2023年末を起点
に算出
（出所） 米労働省、セントルイス連銀より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

米国：生産は底堅く推移。AI・防衛が投資財生産をけん引、半導体増産も寄与
 2026年8月の鉱工業生産は前年比＋1.4％と底堅い結果
― 半導体関連の生産が引き続きけん引。生産能力が2022年対比1.7倍に拡大するなど生産体制の増強が背景。事実、主
要半導体メーカーによる工場新設・増強を受け、MOSロジック（GPU、CPUなど演算用）の量産が本格化
 AI・防衛分野を中心に投資財生産も堅調さを維持
― 投資関連財はAI・防衛が押し上げる形で同＋4.7％と増勢継続。国防・宇宙関連設備は同＋6.1％ と全体を上回る伸び。
地政学リスクの高まりを背景に軍用機やミサイル・防空システムなどの生産体制強化が図られている模様
7

生活費
期待より悪い 期待通り 期待より良い
（シェア）
米国：中間選挙を目前に住宅市場は軟調、アフォーダビリティへの不満が共和党への逆風に
 住宅ローン金利は足元で再び上昇
― 借入コストの負担増により、足元の住宅ローン申請件数は減少
 物価上昇が生活コストを押し上げるなか、金利上昇による住宅取得環境の悪化も家計の不満を助長する要因に
― トランプ大統領の生活費への政策対応は約7割が「期待より悪い」と評価しており、支持率は低下基調
―11月に控える中間選挙では、アフォーダビリティへの対応が引き続き主要な論点

8

欧州：G7は原油・ディーゼルの協調放出で合意。米国の輸出制限リスクは後退
9
 G7はIEAを通じ4カ月で1億バレルの石油備蓄放出に合意。初期20日間はディーゼルを前倒し放出
― 背景にディーゼル価格高騰。中間選挙を控えたトランプ大統領は、禁輸も辞さない姿勢を強調（米国は世界需要の4.9％
を供給）。米国からの輸出が欧州の需要を支える一方、米国よりも潤沢な在庫を保有する欧州に圧力をかけた格好
― 欧州は石油製品消費に占めるディーゼル比率が高く、価格高騰を回避する思惑も協調放出の合意を後押し
― 合意ではG7域内でのエネルギー輸出制限回避も確認され、米国によるEU向け禁輸リスクはひとまず後退
石油製品の製品別消費割合の国際比較
（出所）Energy Institute より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
EUの石油製品消費はディーゼル・軽油が中心

### Page 11

欧州：ディーゼル需給逼迫は残存。欧州のインフレ上振れ要因に
10
 ロシアのディーゼル輸出は足元で急減。中東からの輸出減、中国の輸出停止も重なり、世界需要の約6％が失われた格好
―G7の1億バレル放出で当面の供給不安は緩和するものの、構造的な供給不足の解消には至らず
―IEAも、中東からの原油輸出は回復する一方、石油製品の供給制約はなお深刻と指摘。ディーゼル需給の逼迫が続け
ば、輸送・物流コストを通じたインフレ圧力が残存
 欧州では9月HICPが再加速。ディーゼル高が輸送・物流コストを通じて追加的なインフレ上振れ要因となるリスク
―9月消費者物価（HICP）は前年比＋3.8％（前月：同＋3.2％）と伸び加速。エネルギー価格の上昇が主因
今般の備蓄放出量と停止中のディーゼル輸出
（注） 世界需要およびロシア・中国の輸出量は2025年実績。中東は2026年8月実績を参
考に、輸出量を前年の4分の1としてシェアを概算
（出所）EIA、IEA、OPEC、各種報道より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

（出所）Eurostat より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

欧州：EU・中国はハイブリッド車の輸入制限で暫定合意。同様の措置は他の品目に広がる可能性
11
 EUと中国は、貿易不均衡の是正措置について暫定合意。中国からのハイブリッド車輸入は従来見通しの半分以下に
―EUが中国産EVに追加関税を賦課した2024年以降、追加関税の対象とならないハイブリッド車輸入が急増
― 欧州委員会の試算では、今回の措置によって2030年までの4年間で数百万台の輸入を削減見込み。直近では、EUが輸
入するハイブリッド車の約半分を中国車が占めており、域内自動車市場に一定のインパクト
 欧州委員会は、幅広い産業で貿易不均衡を是正する必要性を示唆。同様の措置が他の品目にも広がる可能性
― 欧州委員会のモニタリング対象（輸入増加と価格下落が併存する品目）は、中国からの輸入品が最多

欧州：2027年度予算審議が本格化。生活苦によるデモが拡大する中、財政赤字は高水準
12
 2027年度予算審議が本格化。財政赤字は総じて高止まり
― 一定の防衛費・エネルギー関連支出を免除する特例措置の適用後も、複数の国でEU財政基準で定める赤字幅を超過。

― フランスの学生によるデモを発端に、生活費の高騰や公共サービスの劣化に対する抗議活動は欧州各国に拡大。一部

欧州：長期金利上昇を受け、一部ECB高官は追加利上げの必要性低下を指摘
13
 フランスでは国内政治・財政面の不安から長期金利が上昇。イタリア・ギリシャなど周辺の高債務国にも波及
― フランス国債の対独利回り差は急拡大し、一時欧州債務危機（2012年）以来の水準に。その後、大統領選の有力候補と
目されるルペン氏（国民連合）が財政赤字を縮小する意欲を示し、拡大はひとまず一服
―ECBによる介入（国債買い入れ等）の可能性については、複数の高官が検討段階にないとの見方を示す
 長期金利の上昇は需要を抑制。ECB高官からは追加利上げの必要性が低下するとの見方も
― エネルギー高によるインフレ圧力と金利上昇による景気下押し圧力が併存。ECBの政策判断は一段と難しくなる可能性

中国：7～9月期の実質GDPは＋4.5％。インフラ投資の加速で、通年の成長率目標を確保へ
 7～9月期の実質GDP成長率は前年比＋4.5％。1～9月の累計で同＋4.6％と、通年目標「＋4.5～5.0％」の範囲内
―7～9月期は、純輸出が＋1.8％Ptと成長をけん引する一方、総資本形成は＋0.6％Ptまで縮小。内需の弱さ鮮明
― 中国政府は地方政府専項債の発行枠を追加（2,500億元）。インフラ投資を加速させ、成長率目標の達成を確保へ
 9月の小売は同＋1.1％と、前月（同＋0.4％）から加速。メモリー高騰が通信機器の売上額（同＋31.1％）を押し上げ
― 投資は同▲7.2％（前月：同▲10.6％） 。前年の反動増は限定的で、インフラ投資は同▲10.5％と2ケタ減が続く

― 生産は同＋5.5％（前月：同＋5.2％）。生産量では、産業用ロボット（同＋18.9％）や集積回路（同＋18.4％）が堅調

中国：政府は住宅ローンの利子補給で市場テコ入れ。人民元相場は緩やかな元高が継続
 中国政府が9/28、新規住宅ローン金利の年率1％分を補助する政策を発表。低迷が続く不動産市場をテコ入れ
― 対象物件の購入金額を150万元（3,500万円）以下に限定し、低中所得者層の住宅ローン負担を軽減する
― これにより、余剰在庫に苦しむ地方都市での実需を掘り起こし、市況のさらなる悪化に歯止めをかけたい考え
 人民元相場は、中国の貿易黒字が過去最高水準を記録する中、緩やかに元高が進行し1ドル＝6.6元台に突入
― 高水準の貿易黒字を背景とした人民元買いの実需が強いことから、当面は緩やかな元高が続く見込み
― ただ、米FRBの利上げで米中金利差が拡大しており、さらなる拡大が意識されれば元安に転じる可能性も

日本：旺盛なAI関連需要が製造業の景況感を押し上げ。利益計画は大幅上方修正
16
 大企業の業況判断は製造業が6四半期連続の改善。非製造業は悪化も一時的な悪天候が主因で変調の懸念は低い
― 業種別では、化学、石油・石炭製品、生産用機械等が改善。旺盛なAI関連需要や原油の供給不足懸念の後退等が背景

― とりわけ大企業・製造業では、輸出向け売上高の上方修正が顕著。上期の実績が大幅に上振れたことを反映したもの
― 世界的なAI関連需要の好調持続が見込まれるもと、下期も堅調な輸出が続けば、利益計画は一段の上方修正余地

― 積み上がった受注残を段階的に消化することで、設備投資が持続的に下支えされる公算が大きい

日本：コメ価格下落で企業物価は鈍化。もっとも、先行きは原油高を背景に再加速へ
18
 9月の国内企業物価はコメ価格の下落を主因に、前年比＋6.8％（前月：同＋7.6％）と鈍化
― 新米価格が昨年を下回ったこと等を背景に、コメ価格が大幅に下落。国内企業物価を▲0.75%Pt押し下げ
― 一方、3月来の原油高を背景に化学・プラスチック製品は高止まり。また、AI需要等を背景に非鉄金属も高い伸びを維持
 7月以降の原油高再燃を背景に、先行きの国内企業物価は再加速する見込み
― 原油価格高止まりにタンカー運賃の急騰が重なり、先行きの輸入物価は原油関連を中心に加速へ
― 国内企業物価は石油・石炭製品やラグを伴いつつ化学・プラスチック製品などを中心に再加速する見込み
（注） コメは玄米及び精米。食品関連は、飲食料品及び農林水産物（除くコメ）

米国債券市場：インフレ加速懸念で上昇後、AI関連株の下落を受け金利は低下
 10月の米10年国債利回りは、一時5.3％台まで上昇したものの、足元では5.0％前後まで低下
― 月初は、原油高等によるインフレ加速懸念を受け、長期金利は約24年ぶりの高水準となる5.3％台まで上昇
― 中旬以降は、AI関連株の先行きに対する慎重な見方から、安全資産である米国債への需要が拡大し、長期金利は低下
 今後も、中東情勢に伴う原油価格の変動やAI関連株の動向など、金利を左右する不確実性が多く、振れの大きい展開が続
く可能性

国内債券・為替：株安を背景に長期金利は低下。ドル円は150円台後半で横ばい圏
 長期金利は、株安を背景に低下。10年金利・30年金利とも、10月上旬から30bps以上の大幅低下
― 米新興クラウドサービスの社債価格急落が株安の契機となり、安全資産とされる日本国債に資金が流入
― 先行き、株式市場の混乱が続けば、短期的には一段の金利低下余地も。もっとも、日銀は四半期に一度のペースで利上
げを続けるとみられ、混乱一巡後の円金利は、再び緩やかな上昇基調に転じる見通し
 ドル円は150円台後半で方向感に欠ける展開。先行きも、150円台を中心とした円安水準が続く見込み
― 原油高は円安要因の一方、株安に伴うリスクオフや仏情勢悪化を受けたユーロ安は円高要因。目先は両者が綱引きへ

内外株式：AIインフラ投資への信用不安の高まりから、AI・半導体関連株を中心に下落

― 企業業績への期待から、10月初の日米株は上昇。S&P500は一時最高値を更新。日経平均も一時7万円台を回復
― その後は、米新興クラウドサービスの社債価格の急落を受け、AI・半導体関連株を中心に下落。原油価格の上昇に伴

うインフレ懸念も下押し圧力に。もっとも、S&P500はヘルスケア関連株等への資金シフトが進み、底堅く推移
 AIインフラ投資に対する信用不安は残存。原油価格の上昇も重石となり、日米株は軟調な推移が続くと予想
― 金利が高水準で推移する中、AIインフラの過剰投資懸念が強まり株価を下押しするリスクには留意が必要

1

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國經濟維持溫和擴張，勞動市場降溫與通膨黏著牽動聯準會政策 
經濟 
⚫ 2026 年第 2 季美國實質 GDP 季增年率終值上修至 2.2%， 惟低於第1 季
之 2.5%。成長主要由個人消費及固定投資帶動，其中消費貢獻 2.51 個
百分點；惟進口大增 12.6%，致淨出口拖累 1.10 個百分點。另實質私人
國內最終銷售成長 4.6%，顯示內需仍具韌性。 
⚫ 9 月美國非農就業僅增 2.9 萬人，遠低於前月及近一年平均，失業率升
至 4.2%， 薪資增幅續降 ， 顯示企業聘僱動能轉弱 。 惟勞動市場尚未明顯
惡化，後續仍須關注就業降溫對聯準會利率決策之影響。 
⚫ 8 月美國職位空缺數由 733.5 萬個降至 707.9 萬個，職缺率降至 4.3%，
顯示企業徵才需求略為降溫；惟聘僱人數增至 519.2 萬人。主動離職及
裁員規模同步下降，反映勞工轉職意願與企業人力調整趨於審慎，勞動
市場供需大致維持平衡。 
⚫ 9 月美國 ISM 製造業採購經理人指數為 54.5，連續第九個月維持擴張。
新訂單、就業及未完成訂單同步改善，顯示需求仍具韌性；惟生產、出
口及進口動能略為放緩，且原物料成本持續攀升，加上關稅政策、地緣
政治及供應鏈不確定性仍存，製造業仍面臨部分壓力。 
⚫ 8 月美國 PCE 物價指數月增 0.3%、年增 3.4%，核心 PCE 月增 0.2%、
年增 3.0%， 顯示通膨大致持穩但短期物價壓力略升 。 另一方面 ， 個人消
費支出月增 0.9%、實質消費成長 0.6%，帶動儲蓄率降至 4.1%，顯示家
庭需求仍具韌性；後續須關注消費熱度及通膨對聯準會政策之影響。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 近期聯準會官員立場趨於分歧，理事 Michael Barr 認為能源價格及 AI
投資熱潮可能延緩通膨降溫 ， 未來仍可能進一步升息 ； 惟John Williams、
Philip Jefferson 及 Michelle Bowman 等官員則主張暫無急於升息的必要 ，
傾向觀察後續經濟數據。加以 9 月非農就業明顯放緩，市場對 10 月升
息預期大幅降溫，焦點轉向 12 月會議。 
⚫ 台積電傳評估與馬斯克旗下 Terafab 合作赴德州設廠，雙方仍處討論階
段。合作可能由台積電主導營運，或由 Terafab 持多數股權、台積電提
供技術 。 若成真 ， 將擴大台積電美國布局 ， 並支援Tesla、SpaceX 及 xAI
晶片需求。

2

短評 
⚫ 美國經濟目前仍維持溫和擴張，內需與投資表現優於預期，短期衰退風
險有限 ； 惟近期新增就業明顯低於預期 ， 企業聘僱意願與職缺需求放緩 ，
勞動市場動能已有轉弱跡象，而通膨受能源價格、 AI 投資及關稅等因
素影響仍具黏著性。在經濟成長放緩與通膨尚未完全受控的環境下，聯
準會短期可能傾向維持觀望，惟若通膨再度升溫，不排除年底前仍有進
一步升息空間。 
歐 
元 
區 
法國殖利率大幅上升，不利於投資及消費表現 
經濟 
⚫ 歐元區 9 月 HICP 通膨、核心通膨(不含食品及能源)分別上升 0.6、0.1
個百分點至 3.8%、2.5%。 
⚫ 歐元區 8 月失業率持平 6.4%。 
⚫ 歐元區第二季家庭儲蓄率下降 0.2 個百分點至 14.2%。 
⚫ 歐元區第二季房價年增率為 4%，較第一季降 0.6 個百分點。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 法國 10 年期公債殖利率升至 4.9%以上，與德國公債殖利率差距擴大至
125 個基點以上。 
短評 ⚫ 法國財政前景不明朗 ， 未來仍可能面臨殖利率進一步上升與財政壓力加劇的風險，暫時不利於消費及投資表現。 
日 
本 
景氣溫和復甦，但消費放緩與通膨升溫風險升高 
經濟 
⚫ 8 月工業生產月減 1.7%。 
⚫ 8 月零售月減 1.2%。 
⚫ 9 月東京核心通膨年增 2.7%。 
短評 
⚫ 儘管消費動能放緩與通膨壓力升溫 ， 但企業投資與生產回升趨勢仍具韌
性，經濟可望維持溫和復甦，惟日本央行進一步升息的可能性正逐步提
高。 
中 
國 
為刺激內需、引導產業走向，人行漸近式放寬政策工具 
經濟 ⚫ 9 月官方製造業 PMI、非製造業 PMI 分別上升為 50.1、50.2。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 人行於 9 月 29 日宣布下調抵押補充貸款(該貸款本質是將資金引導至政
府希望發展的領域)利率自 1.75%降至 1.50%，並擴大適用範圍。 
短評 
⚫ 未來若內需復甦力道不如預期 ， 不排除人行將進一步降準或擴大結構性
貨幣工具支持經濟 ； 至於全面降息則仍須兼顧人民幣匯率及中美利差等
因素，可能性相對較低。

國際經濟金融週報 
兆豐國際商業銀行 徵信處 
115.10.5 
一、 美國—美國經濟維持溫和擴張，勞動市場降溫與通膨黏著牽動聯準會政策 
2026 年第二季美國實質 GDP 季增年率終值由前次估計之 1.5%上修至 2.2%，
惟仍低於第一季之 2.5%， 顯示經濟持續擴張但成長動能略有放緩 。 此次上修主要
反映投資、消費及政府支出優於原先估計。就成長結構觀察，個人消費支出仍為
主要動能，貢獻 2.51 個百分點，其中服務及商品消費分別貢獻 1.57 及 0.94 個百
分點；私人投資貢獻 0.82 個百分點，以設備投資及智慧財產權投資表現強勁，惟
庫存調整拖累 0.53 個百分點。對外貿易方面，出口成長 5.0%，但進口大幅增加
12.6%， 致淨出口拖累GDP 成長 1.10 個百分點 。 另反映內需基本面的實質私人國
內最終銷售成長 4.6%， 高於第一季之1.8%， 顯示民間需求明顯增強 。 物價方面 ，
第 2 季 PCE 及核心 PCE 物價指數分別上升 5.0%及 3.3%， 顯示通膨壓力仍偏高 。
整體而言，美國經濟仍由消費與固定投資支撐，惟進口快速增加及通膨黏著性，
仍為後續經濟前景的重要觀察重點。 
2026 年 9 月美國非農就業人數增加 2.9 萬人 ， 明顯低於上月修正後的13.3 萬
人，也低於過去 12 個月平均每月增加 4.5 萬人的水準；失業率小幅升至 4.2%，
整體仍維持在 3 月以來 4.1%至 4.3%的區間，反映勞動市場整體變化有限，惟新
增就業力道已轉趨疲弱。家庭調查方面，勞動參與率升至 61.8%，就業人口比為
59.2%，均較上月小幅上升；長期失業人數約 194.4 萬人，占總失業人口 27.1%，
大致維持穩定。產業別方面，主要產業就業變化有限，其中醫療保健增加 1.7 萬
人、營建業增加 1.1 萬人、製造業增加 0.9 萬人，金融活動則減少 0.7 萬人，政府
部門減少 1.7 萬人。薪資方面，私人部門平均時薪升至 37.81 美元，月增 0.1%、
年增 3.0%，平均每週工時維持 34.4 小時。另 7 月與 8 月新增就業合計下修 6 萬
人，反映前二月就業動能較初估疲弱。整體而言，9 月美國新增就業明顯放緩、
失業率小幅上升且薪資增速降溫，企業聘僱動能較先前轉弱；惟失業率仍維持於
近期區間，勞動市場尚未出現明顯惡化跡象，後續仍須關注企業聘僱需求是否持
續降溫，以及對聯準會利率決策的影響。 
2026 年 8 月美國職位空缺數(Job Openings) 由上月修正後之 733.5 萬個減少
25.6 萬個至 707.9 萬個 ， 職缺率由4.4%降至 4.3%， 顯示企業用人需求略有降溫 ；

### Page 6

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引
用相關內容。

5

惟聘僱人數由 514.6 萬人小幅增加至 519.2 萬人，聘僱率由 3.2%升至 3.3%。總離
職人數由 512.8 萬人減少至 507.0 萬人，離職率維持於 3.2%。就離職結構觀察，
主動離職(Quits)人數由 308.9 萬人降至 306.6 萬人，主動離職率維持 1.9%；裁員
與解僱(Layoffs and Discharges) 人數則由 170.2 萬人降至 164.1 萬人，裁員率由
1.1%降至 1.0%。產業別方面，職位空缺增加主要來自零售業、金融保險業、資訊
業及休閒餐飲業 ； 聘僱人數則以政府部門及製造業增幅較為明顯 。 整體而言 ，8 月
美國職缺數較前月下滑，反映企業徵才需求略有降溫，但聘僱活動仍維持穩定，
且主動離職與裁員規模同步下降，顯示勞工轉職意願及企業人力調整趨於審慎，
勞動市場供需仍大致維持平衡。 
2026 年 9 月美國 ISM 製造業採購經理人指數(Manufacturing PMI) 為 54.5，
較上月之 54.6 微降 0.1 點，惟已連續第九個月維持於 50 以上擴張區間，顯示製
造業景氣持續擴張。細項觀察，新訂單指數由53.7 升至 55.3，需求動能續增；生
產指數由 58.3 降至 56.7，雖仍維持擴張，但成長速度略有放緩；就業指數由51.2
升至 52.7，反映企業聘僱需求持續增加。供應商交貨指數由 59.3 微降至 59.0，交
貨延遲情況仍存；庫存指數則由 50.6 降至 48.6，企業備貨態度轉趨保守。此外，
價格指數由 71.1 升至 77.9，原物料及投入成本壓力升高；未完成訂單指數由51.8
升至 56.4，訂單去化變慢。另一方面，新出口訂單指數由53.2 降至 50.9，進口指
數亦由 52.5 降至 51.0，外需成長動能及企業備料需求略為放緩。整體而言，9 月
美國製造業活動持續穩健擴張，新訂單、就業及未完成訂單同步改善，顯示製造
業景氣仍具韌性；惟出口及進口增速放緩、投入成本壓力升高，加上關稅政策、
地緣政治風險及供應鏈不確定性仍存，後續發展仍值得持續關注。 
2026 年 8 月美國個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數月增率由上月之 0.1%升至 0.3%，
年增率維持於 3.4%； 扣除食品及能源後之核心PCE 月增率由 0.1%升至 0.2%，年
增率亦持平於 3.0%，顯示整體通膨大致持穩，惟短期物價漲勢較前月略有回升。
另一方面，個人所得月增 0.2%，可支配所得(DPI)月增 0.3%，實質可支配所得持
平；個人消費支出月增 0.9%，實質個人消費支出成長 0.6%，顯示民間消費表現
明顯回溫。由於消費增幅高於所得增幅，個人儲蓄率由前月之 4.6%降至 4.1%。
整體而言，8 月美國通膨表現大致持穩，但消費支出顯著回升，反映家庭需求仍
具韌性；後續仍須持續關注消費熱度、通膨走勢及其對聯準會貨幣政策路徑之影
響。

近期聯準會官員談話呈現鷹鴿分歧。聯準會理事 Michael Barr 認為，中東局
勢推升能源價格，以及 AI 相關投資熱潮帶動需求增長，均可能阻礙通膨回落至
2%目標 ， 未來仍不排除進一步升息的必要性 ； 惟 紐約聯準銀行總裁 John Williams、
聯準會副主席 Philip Jefferson 及金融監理副主席 Michelle Bowman 則強調，在 9
月升息後，當前並無急迫性立即進一步收緊政策，應先觀察後續通膨、就業及金
融市場數據變化，再評估是否有進一步調整利率的必要。受官員談話轉趨審慎，
以及 9 月非農就業表現疲弱影響，市場對 10 月 FOMC 會議再度升息的預期明顯
降溫，政策焦點逐漸轉向 12 月會議及年底前是否仍有一次升息空間。 
晶圓代工龍頭台積電傳出正評估與馬斯克旗下 Terafab 合作，在美國德州布
局晶圓廠，馬斯克也於社群平台 X 證實雙方正在討論，並表示 「目前僅在討論階
段 ， 但可能會有進展」 。 目前浮上檯面的合作模式包括由台積電持有並營運新廠 ，
SpaceX 或 Terafab 透過入股及承諾採購晶圓參與；另一方案則由 Terafab 掌握多
數股權 ， 台積電提供製程技術與晶圓廠營運經驗 。Terafab 旨在因應 Tesla、SpaceX
及 xAI 快速成長的 AI 晶片需求，首期投資規模達 168 億美元。若合作成真，不
僅可望成為台積電繼亞利桑那州後在美國的新布局，也凸顯先進製程與穩定晶片
供應的重要性，但目前具體合作方式、時程與投資內容仍未正式定案。 
二、歐元區—法國殖利率大幅上升，不利於投資及消費表現 
 歐元區 9 月 HICP 通膨 、 核心通膨(不含食品及能源)分別上升 0.6、0.1 個百分
點至 3.8%、2.5%， 主因國際原油價格上漲 ， 使能源通膨上升4.5 個百分點至 18.8%，
並透過肥料、物流成本上漲，間接推升食品通膨 0.3 個百分點至 1.4%。另外，服
務通膨上升 0.2 個百分點至 3.2%，近三個月服務通膨大致呈現橫向波動，部分反
映旅遊旺季因素影響。基於布蘭特原油持續位於每桶 90 美元以上，預期年底前
仍將對通膨帶來額外壓力，後續須關注能源漲價可能會透過運輸、物流等成本管
道傳導至核心商品及服務價格，進而提高核心通膨黏性。 
 其他經濟數據包含 ：(1)歐元區 8 月失業率持平 6.4%， 失業人數增加2.6 萬人 ，
主要是義大利勞動參與人數增加所致，不過 ，基於PMI 顯示近期德國勞動雇用需
求持續增溫，將有助於穩定勞動市場表現；(2)歐元區第二季家庭儲蓄率下降 0.2
個百分點至 14.2%，顯示通膨壓力削弱民眾購買力，致儲蓄暫時性減少；(3)歐元
區第二季房價年增率為 4%， 較第一季降0.6 個百分點 ， 主因法國中古屋面臨嚴格

環保法規，須負擔高昂翻修成本，使法國房價呈現連三季下滑所拖累。 
 近一週法國 10 年期公債殖利率升至 4.9%以上，與德國公債殖利率差距擴大
至 125 個基點以上，主要反映市場對法國主權公債可持續性疑慮升溫。儘管目前
政府計畫將 2027 年財政赤字縮限至 5%，惟政府在國會並無穩定多數席次，即使
能以行政命令強行通過預算 ， 也可能面臨倒閣風險 ；此外 ，市場對於2027 年 4 月
總統大選後，新總統能否取得國會足夠支持，推動削減赤字與控制債務的改革亦
缺乏信心。因此未來法國仍可能面臨殖利率進一步上升與財政壓力加劇的風險，
暫時不利於投資及消費信心。 
三、日本—景氣溫和復甦，但消費放緩與通膨升溫風險升高 
 8 月工業生產月減 1.7%，主要係受到該月地震及豪雨等天然災害影響，導致
部分工廠暫時停產。從細項來看，汽車業受停工影響最為明顯，產量下滑6.8%，
電子產品產量亦減少 1.9%。 惟生產機械產量連續三個月成長 ， 加上核心資本財出
貨持續增加，顯示企業投資需求仍相當強勁，有助於支撐後續工業生產回升。從
企業預期來看，製造商預期9 月產量將成長 3.2%，顯示生產活動可望迅速回溫，
在供應鏈逐步恢復、庫存回補需求持續，以及穩健的企業資本支出支撐下，預計
第三季工業生產仍可維持正成長，且成長動能可望延續至年底。 
 8 月零售月減 1.2%，雖然表現略優於市場預期，但仍顯示民間消費動能正在
放緩。此次疲弱主要原因來自耐久財銷售下滑，其中家電銷售因空調等產品在換
機需求告一段落後明顯回落，汽車銷售也隨政府刺激措施效應逐漸消退而連續第
四個月下降。相較之下，食品、服裝等非耐久財銷售雖同步走弱，但降幅相對有
限 ， 反映薪資成長仍對消費提供部分支撐 。惟不利天候與通膨壓力仍是主要風險 ，
8 月至 9 月持續豪雨已影響服裝等非必需消費，而除米價以外的食品及商品價格
持續上漲，也正侵蝕家庭實質購買力，恐限制消費者信心與消費復甦力道。 
 9 月東京核心通膨年增率升至 2.7%，雖然東京都免費托兒及免費水費政策造
成的基期效應貢獻約 0.5 個百分點，但通膨升溫主因仍是價格上漲已由能源與食
品擴散至整體經濟。受石油價格上揚帶動，企業開始加速將原物料及薪資成本轉
嫁給消費者，帶動商品與服務價格普遍走高；租金通膨更升至 2%，創 1994 年以
來新高，公共交通及醫療等項目也開始調漲。隨著東京的物價壓力逐步反映至全
國數據，日本央行進一步升息的可能性升高。

四、中國—為刺激內需、引導產業走向，人行漸近式放寬政策工具 
9 月官方製造業 PMI 上升 0.3 點至 50.1，為近三個月高點，新訂單、出口訂
單指數分別為 50.5、50，並帶動生產指數上升至 51.7，不過，庫存指數下降、就
業指數加速衰退、企業展望減弱，皆拖累 PMI 指數水準。此外，投入、產出物價
指數分別上升至 60.8、54，顯示成本壓力加劇，但售價調整速度依然較為遲緩，
不利於獲利表現。官方非製造業 PMI 上升 1.2 點至 50.2，其中建築業活動指數大
增 3.4 點至 50.3，脫離連續八個月的衰退區間，主要是政府加大財政刺激力度，
推動重大建設開工；非製造業的從業人員指數為 45.9，顯示就業情況仍顯嚴峻，
亦較不利於內需表現。 
鑒於內需動能疲弱，人行於 9 月 29 日宣布下調抵押補充貸款(該貸款本質是
將資金引導至政府希望發展的領域)利率自 1.75%降至 1.50%，並擴大適用範圍，
將水網、新型電網、算力網、新一代通信網、城市地下管網及物流網等建設納入
支持，有助於提振內需動能。未來若內需復甦力道不如預期，不排除人行將進一
步降準或擴大結構性貨幣工具支持經濟；至於全面降息則仍須兼顧人民幣匯率及
中美利差等因素，可能性相對較低。

### Page 1

Nien Made Enterprise

3Q preview: EPS +13% YoY to NT$6.6; 
Better 2H but not yet a turnaround

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 441.00 TWD | Price: 358.50 TWD

3Qe EPS NT$6.6: strong rev and GP offset by opex and FX 
Nien Made is scheduled to report 3Q26 results on 4 Nov. Disclosed monthly sales imply 
3Q26 revenue growth of 16% YoY to NT$8.5bn, or 9% in USD terms, largely 
volume-driven. Growth reaccelerated from 1.7% YoY in 1H26, led by custom-made on a 
low US base and expansion in Europe and Japan. Ready-made also improved as 
Cambodia labor shortages eased. (See Exhibit 1 for US window coverings spending 
trends). Gross margin: We expect underlying GPM to rise 1.1ppt YoY to 59.8% on lower 
tariffs, broadly flat QoQ. We forecasts an NT$300mn of IEEPA tariff refunds in 2H26. 
Assuming NT$150mn is booked in 3Q, this would add 1.7ppt and lift reported GPM to 
61.5%. We expect the selling expense ratio to fall 1.7ppt QoQ to 17.5% on lower fuel 
charges, but remain 0.5ppt higher YoY due to continued salesforce and distribution 
network investment. We expect an FX loss in 3Q26 vs. an NT$118mn gain in 3Q25, 
weighing on YoY earnings growth. We forecast 3Q26 EPS of NT$6.6, up 13% YoY. 
US window covering picking up, but no turnaround yet  
BofA credit/debit card data suggests US spending on window coverings turned positive 
in Aug-26 for the first time after 19 consecutive months of YoY declines. Spending rose 
4%/3% YoY in Aug/Sep, respectively. This improvement partly explains the recent 
reacceleration in Nien Made's monthly sales. That said, we view the improvement more 
as normalization off a low base than the start of an industry turnaround. The Aug-Sep 
2025 comparison base was relatively weak, while the current rate-hike environment 
remains a headwind to housing-related demand. 
Slightly raise FY26 EPS est. to NT$22.9; PO to NT$441 
We raise our FY26 EPS estimate by 2% to NT$22.9 to reflect the blended impact of 
stronger-than-expected revenue growth and FX loss. We maintain our FY26 DPS forecast 
of NT$17 per share. We raise our PO by 4% to NT$441, as we roll forward our valuation 
basis from FY26E EPS to FY27E EPS. At the same time, we lower our target P/E from 
19x to 17x, reflecting the MSCI removal and a less supportive rate environment.

* For full definitions of iQmethod SM measures, see page 7.

Company Sector 
Interior Furnishings

Company Description 
Nien Made, founded in 1974, is one of the world's largest 
window-covering manufacturers. It has roughly 50% 
market share in both ready-made (OEM business) shades 
and blinds, and custom-made (own brand) shutters in the 
US. The US market accounted for over 75% of sales. Key 
clients include Home Depot and Wal-Mart for ready-made 
shades and blinds. Nien Made operates its own retail 
network and e-commerce business for its custom-made 
shutters under the Norman brand name in the US and 
Europe.

Investment Rationale 
We have Buy rating on Nien Made, mainly given 1) clear 
custom-made growth profile (share gain in the US and fast-
growing DTC in Japan and Europe), 2) its largest customer 
Home Depot is gaining market share, and 3) margin 
expansion potentials backed by favorable products mix.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
Nien Made Enterprise (XNRBF) 
Our PO NT$441 is derived from 17x P/E 2027E EPS. This is around 0.5SD above 
historical the one-year forward P/E, which we think is warranted given the visibility of 
the growth profile.

Downside risks are 1) weaker-than-expected Home Depot's sell-through, 2) slow 
recovery of US housing turnover, and 3) higher tariff increases on China exports to the 
US. 4) Loss of key customers.

 Measures Definitions 
Business Performance Numerator Denominator 
Return On Capital Employed NOPAT = (EBIT + Interest Income) × (1 − Tax Rate) + Goodwill Amortization Total Assets − Current Liabilities + ST Debt + Accumulated Goodwill 
Amortization 
Return On Equity Net Income Shareholders’ Equity 
Operating Margin Operating Profit Sales 
Earnings Growth Expected 5 Year CAGR From Latest Actual N/A 
Free Cash Flow Cash Flow From Operations − Total Capex N/A  
Quality of Earnings Numerator Denominator 
Cash Realization Ratio Cash Flow From Operations Net Income 
Asset Replacement Ratio Capex Depreciation 
Tax Rate Tax Charge Pre-Tax Income 
Net Debt-To-Equity Ratio Net Debt = Total Debt − Cash & Equivalents Total Equity 
Interest Cover EBIT Interest Expense  
Valuation Toolkit Numerator Denominator 
Price / Earnings Ratio Current Share Price Diluted Earnings Per Share (Basis As Specified) 
Price / Book Value Current Share Price Shareholders’ Equity / Current Basic Shares 
Dividend Yield Annualised Declared Cash Dividend Current Share Price 
Free Cash Flow Yield Cash Flow From Operations − Total Capex Market Cap = Current Share Price × Current Basic Shares 
Enterprise Value / Sales EV = Current Share Price × Current Shares + Minority Equity + Net Debt +

Sales 
EV / EBITDA Enterprise Value Basic EBIT + Depreciation + Amortization 
iQmethod SMis the set of BofA Global Research standard measures that serve to maintain global consistency under three broad headings: Business Performance, Quality of Earnings, and validations. The key features of 
iQmethod are: A consistently structured, detailed, and transparent methodology. Guidelines to maximize the effectiveness of the comparative valuation process, and to identify some common pitfalls. 
iQdatabase ®  is our real-time global research database that is sourced directly from our equity analysts’ earnings models and includes forecasted as well as historical data for income statements, balance sheets, and cash 
flow statements for companies covered by BofA Global Research. 
iQprofile SM, iQmethod SM  are service marks of Bank of America Corporation. iQdatabase ® is a registered service mark of Bank of America Corporation.

Powerchip Semiconductor Manufacturing Co  | Asia
Pacific
Margin beat; pricing catch-up
continues
3Q26 earnings beat:
• Revenue was NT$21.34bn, +23% Q/Q, +80% Y/Y, 2% below our estimate.
• Gross profit was NT$10.1bn, GM was 47.5%, +19.3ppt Q/Q, +54.1ppt Y/Y,
21.2ppt above our estimate
• EPS was NT$1.38, +82% Q/Q, 84% above our estimate.
• 3Q26 recorded NT$660mn of opex associated with the Tongluo fab
(reclassified from COGS).
Other takeaways:

• AI packaging remains an execution watchpoint – 3D AI foundry's revenue
decreased from 5.4% of the mix in 2Q to 1.4% in 3Q during the relocation,
although interposers are expected to be fully loaded by year-end.
• IPD offers structural growth potential. Management estimates that  industry-
wide 30-50kwpm high capacitance demand for 2H27 and  onward far exceeds
PSMC's planned capacity of 8-10kwpm.
• PWF mass production is targeted for 4Q27, with revenue growth

Key Takeaways
Management expects double-digit Q/Q sales growth in 4Q26.

catch-up over the four-month production cycle.

negotiations.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Powerchip Semiconductor Manufacturing Co (6770.TW)
Base case, 2.0x 2027e P/B. This method is in line with our approach for Greater China memory
IDM players, in view of the industry's high volatility. Our 2.0x target P/B is higher than the
historical average of 1.5x since 2021, reflecting our optimistic view on improved pricing and
utilization.
Risks to Upside
n Pricing power is sustained in 2026.
n Listing on main board attracts investment fund flows.

n Aluminum process enjoys higher margins.
Risks to Downside
n Competition from China intensifies.
n Price erosion is faster than expected.
n Demand from consumer electronics clients weakens further.
n Inventory in the supply chain takes longer to digest.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

研究員：陳泳如 
kate401124-71795@esunsec.com.tw

法 說 前 瞻               產業：封測 
日月光法說會前財務預估 
4Q26 Full Process 放量帶動公司獲利持續轉佳

| 財報訊息   
日月光即將於10/29(四)舉行法說會，市場普遍預估4Q26營收有望季
增約中高個位數，且毛利率受惠於LEAP業務持續成長挹注ATM獲
利，有望進一步攀升；另關注公司對於2027年LEAP營收、ATM獲
利結構有無進一步更新。 
| 營運現況  
3Q26營收優於公司財測：3Q26營收季增27.8%至2,441.6億元，優於
公司先前財測 (估營收季增 21~22%)，主因為 ATM業務大幅優於預
期，另EMS表現也微幅優於預期，兩者成長動能中， ATM受惠於
AI/HPC需求強勁和封測報價調漲，EMS則因蘋果手機季節性備貨、
記憶體成本轉嫁之挹注。我們預估毛利率21.4%、稅後EPS 5.75元。  
| 營運展望  
Full Process 放量帶動LEAP營收、獲利進一步成長：我們預估本季
營收有望季增4.8%至2,557.6億元，約落在市場共識之下緣，主要成
長動能來自於AMD伺服器CPU Venice帶動Full Process放量，且進一
步改善ATM獲利結構。展望明年，我們觀察：(1)封測產業產能利用
率維持高檔、AI/HPC需求維持強勁，持續支撐封測報價存在漲價空
間，且明年Full Process在AMD伺服器CPU、博通交換器晶片等訂單
挹注下 ， 維持高速成長 ， 預估明年LEAP營收將由今年的37億美元成
長至74億美元以上(公司先前已提到明年LEAP營收將翻倍)；(2)EMS
雖面臨消費性電子需求不彰，但於3Q26漲價後，明年營收將有全年
之漲價貢獻。

## Document 200: [005380] 현대차(005380) 4분기 반등 기대현대차(005380) 4분기 반등 기대4분기 반등 기대 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005380] 현대차(005380) 4분기 반등 기대현대차(005380) 4분기 반등 기대4분기 반등 기대 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-16
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

현대차 (005380)

EARNINGS PREVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.10.16  
4분기 반등 기대

3Q26 Preview 
현대차는 3Q26E 매출액 42.4조원(-9.4% YoY, -13.9% QoQ), 영업이익 2.5조원(-
2.8% YoY, -13.5% QoQ, OPM 5.8%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회할 전망이다. 각 부문
별 영업이익은 자동차 1.7조원(-2.0% YoY, -14.9% QoQ, OPM 4.8%), 금융 5,501억
원(-4.5% YoY, -27.5% QoQ, OPM 6.9%)으로 예상한다. 
생산 정상화 및 신차 확대를 통한 수익성 개선 기대 
현대차는 글로벌 도매 판매 91.8만대(-11.5% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 90.9
만대(-9.6%)를 기록했다. 7~8월에 발생한 파업 영향으로 약 5.5만대 규모의 생산 차
질이 발생했다. 파업 영향으로 HEV 판매 증가율이 1%에 그치면서, HEV 판매 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선 효과도 시장 기대에 미치지 못했다.  
4분기부터는 3분기 파업 영향으로 지연된 생산량 회복 및 신차 확대가 본격화되면서, 
전분기 대비 매출 및 수익성 개선이 기대된다. 동사는 보급형 전기차 아이오닉3의 튀
르키예 생산을 9월부터 시작했으며, 이를 통해 유럽 시장 판매량은 점차 회복될 전망
이다. 또한, 1) HMGMA를 통한 HEV 미국 현지 생산 확대, 2) 제네시스 HEV 모델 순
차적 출시를 통해 내년부터 북미를 중심으로 믹스 개선이 본격화될 전망이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 520,000원으로 하향 
2027년 영업이익은 11.2조원(+10.2%)를 기록할 것으로 예상된다. 원화 약세 지속을 
반영해 이익 추정치를 하향하며, 이에 따라 목표주가를 하향한다. 다만, 현재 주가는 
12M Fwd P/E 기준 6.1배로 Legacy OEM 평균(7.7배)보다 낮은 수준이다. 1) RMAC 
성과 구체화, 2) RA(로봇 생산법인) 착공 등 Physical AI 사업이 구체화될 경우 밸류에
이션 할증이 가능할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr 
Buy (유지)

## Document 242: [402340] SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대하이닉스 자사주 소각_31fbe439

Source PDF: all report pdf/[402340] SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대하이닉스 자사주 소각_31fbe439.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

SK 스퀘어 (402340/KS) 
하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대 
예정보다 빠른 SK 하이닉스 자사주 매입 
SK스퀘어의 자회사인 SK하이닉스는 지난 8월 20일부터 11월 19일까지 자사주 40
조원(2,407만주, 3.3%)을 매입 예정이다. 9월 29일까지 1,715만주, 예정 매입수량 
대비 71.3%를 매입 완료하였다. 매입 초기 매일 65만주 매입하다가 9월 10일부터는 
60만주를 매입하고 있는데, 이러한 추세가 유지된다면 10월 중순경 자사주 매입이 마
무리될 예정이다. 자사주 매입 후 소각 예정으로 소각이 완료되면 SK스퀘어의 SK하아
닉스에 대한 지분율은 20.0%에서 20.7%로 상승하게 된다.

자사주 매입 이후 추가적인 주주환원에 대한 기대 
SK하이닉스는 지난 8월 19일 정책기간(‘25년~’27년) 동안 발생하는 누적 FCF의 
50% 이상을 주주환원 하는 주주환원 정책을 발표한 바 있다. 26년 SK하이닉스의 예
상 FCF는 164조원(컨센서스 기준)으로 50%를 주주환원 할 경우 자사주 매입 금액
(40 조원)을 제외한 주주환원 재원은 42 조원이다. 잔여 주주환원 재원이 모두 배당으
로 지급된다면 SK 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후 SK 스퀘어의 지분율은 20.7%이므로 
SK스퀘어의 배당수입도 8.7조원에 달한다. 지난 3월 SK스퀘어는 ‘26년~’28년 경
상배당수입의 30% 이상을 주주에게 환원하는 정책을 발표했다. 보수적으로 30%만 
가정하더라도 SK스퀘어의 배당재원은 2.6조원(현 시가총액 대비 1.8%)이다. SK스퀘
어는 연초 주주총회에서 배당가능이익 확보 차원에서 자본준비금 5 조 8,900억원을 
이익잉여금으로 전입한 바 있다. 이는 26년 재무제표가 확정되는 27년 3월 이후 배당
가능이익으로 편입되므로 SK스퀘어의 주주환원 확대가 기대된다.

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 185 만원(유지) 
SK스퀘어의 순자산 가치 중 98% 이상이 SK하이닉스 지분가치이다. SK하이닉스는 
자사주 매입 이후 17.7% 주가가 상승했으나, SK 스퀘어 주가는 11.4% 상승했다. SK
하이닉스 자사주 매입·소각은 SK 스퀘어의 보유 지분가치 상승뿐 아니라 지분율도 상
승하기 때문에 자사주가 소각될 경우 SK스퀘어의 주가가 탄력적으로 움직일 가능성이 
높다. SK스퀘어 현재 NAV 대비 할인율은 43.7%, 12MF PBR 1.4배로 7월 이후 주
가 조정으로 밸류에이션 매력도 높아졌다. 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 185만원을 유지
한다.

## Document 247: AI“完美风暴”剧本：债市“吹响号角”，财报“雪上加霜”，中选“最后一击”？

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI“完美风暴”剧本：债市“吹响号角”，财报“雪上加霜”，中选“最后一击”？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-01
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

AI 热潮遭遇债市、财报与政治⻛险三重夹击：融资⻔槛抬⾼， AI 云公司债券市场近乎关
闭；科技巨头资本开⽀激增、⾃由现⾦流恶化，股价承压；中期选举临近，数据中⼼反对
声浪或推动监管收紧。市场正从追逐算⼒转向拷问投资回报。
摘要：
美国 AI 扩张正在进⼊⼀个更严苛阶段：资本不再只为增⻓叙事买单，债市、财报和政治⻛险同时
逼近，投资者开始要求 AI 投资证明其回报能⼒。
贷款⼈和投资者正变得更挑剔，并要求更严格条款。即便是 SpaceX 和 Meta 等⼤型科技相关发⾏
⼈的债券也在⼤幅折价交易， CoreWeave ， Nebius 和 Nscale 等 AI 云公司近期实际上已被挡在债
券市场⻔外。
财报季未能缓和疑虑，反⽽放⼤了市场对 AI 资本开⽀和⾃由现⾦流的担忧。 Nasdaq 100 指数 10
⽉下跌 6.7% ，市值蒸发 2.7 万亿美元。 Alphabet 股价在财报后单⽇暴跌 13% ，创下该公司史上最
差单⽇表现。
政治⻛险也在升温。随着美国中期选举临近，数据中⼼建设引发的电价、环境和监管争议正在

进⼊市场定价。若⺠调和预测市场显⽰的⺠主党重新掌控国会前景兑现， AI ⾏业可能⾯临更密集
的调查、听证和监管议程。
债市先变脸： AI 融资进⼊买⽅市场
过去⼏年，美国 AI 热潮背后的公司轻松融资数千亿美元。现在，华尔街开始提⾼⻔槛。
投 资 者 担 ⼼ ⼤ 型数 据 中 ⼼ 项 ⽬ 可 能 因 基 层政 治 反 弹 ⽽ 延 误 ， 也 担 ⼼ 超 ⼤ 规 模 云 ⼚ 商 以 及
Anthropic ， OpenAI 等 AI 初创公司的利润⽆法如预期快速兑现。与此同时，股票和债券融资供应
激增，使投资者担忧对 AI 主题的⻛险敞⼝过⾼。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

这种转变已经反映在市场价格中。 AI 相关债券发⾏折价扩⼤，部分发⾏⼈融资窗⼝收窄。 Pimco
⾸席投资官 Dan Ivascyn 表⽰，贷款⼈要求企业证明投资能转化为⾜够收⼊的⻔槛正 “ 越来越
⾼ ” ， “ 薄弱环节会最先承压 ” 。
即便资⾦仍然存在，条款也更贵。 SpaceX 本周宣布完成 400 亿美元融资，⽤于购买 Nvidia 最新
芯⽚，这被称为 AI 芯⽚领域规模最⼤的融资。但纪录仅维持数⼩时， Broadcom 随后敲定 630 亿
美元交易，向 Anthropic 出租芯⽚。即便如此，据《华尔街⽇报》报道， Broadcom ⾸席执⾏官
Hock Tan 仍需在最后阶段亲⾃介⼊谈判，以安抚银⾏疑虑。
新云公司承压：⾼收益债窗⼝⼏近关闭
压⼒最先集中在所谓 neocloud 公司⾝上。这类公司拥有或租赁数据中⼼，部署 AI 服务器，并向
⼤型科技公司、交易公司和 AI 初创公司出售算⼒。它们借款规模⼤，对利率和融资条件变化尤其
敏感。
据 Bank of America 数据，美国企业债市场中 AI 相关发⾏规模在 2025 年为 3940 亿美元，今年截⾄

10 ⽉中旬已达 6550 亿美元。分析师预计，明年新增 AI 相关债务规模将接近 1 万亿美元。
但市场承接能⼒正在被测试。 CoreWeave 今年早些时候以 9.75% 票息发⾏的债券，⽬前⼆级市
场收益率已超过 15% 。这意味着短时间内借款成本显著上升。银⾏家近期警告称， neocloud 公
司的⾼收益债新发⾏市场基本关闭， 15% 以上收益率对借款⽅⽽⾔成本过⾼。
Nebius 是⼀个典型案例。该公司⾃ 2024 年以来向 Nvidia 和其他投资者出售约 27 亿美元股票，并
发⾏数⼗亿美元可转债⽀持 AI 扩张。但据《华尔街⽇报》援引知情⼈⼠报道， Nebius 曾考虑⾸
次发⾏⾼收益债，随后决定不推进。⾸席执⾏官 Arkady Volozh 上周在柏林⼀场会议上表⽰，⼀
些资本来源 “ 暂时关闭 ” ，公司正通过客⼾预付款和其他⼯具筹资。
股权市场也不轻松。 Lambda 在 IPO 前最后⼀轮融资中，将⽬标估值从 145 亿美元下调⾄ 130 亿美
元。 Nscale 原本考虑 11 ⽉上市，⽬前倾向推迟⾄ 12 ⽉。该公司还在与 Nvidia 和其他投资者谈

判，为 Anthropic 位于西弗吉尼亚州的 450 亿美元数据中⼼提供融资⽀持，但潜在⽀持⽅在市场转
向后要求更严格条款。
巨头也遇到集中度约束
⼤型超⼤规模云⼚商的信⽤质量远⾼于 neocloud 公司，但问题在于融资规模过⼤。
Meta ， Amazon ， Alphabet 和 Microsoft 拥有⾼信⽤评级，也有能⼒继续增加债务。但多家公司
今年已⼤量发⾏⾼等级债，并暗⽰未来仍将持续融资，这使投资公司、保险公司和养⽼⾦等传
统买⽅开始担⼼单⼀⾏业和单⼀主题集中度过⾼。
Ivascyn 表⽰，市场⾯临⼀个现实问题：投资者在⼀个名字或⼀个主题上究竟还能买多少。
这种压⼒可能解释了为何部分公司转向股权融资。 Meta 公布计划通过⼀系列股票发⾏最多筹资
975 亿美元。其证券⽂件提到， Alphabet ， SpaceX ， Anthropic 和 OpenAI 等公司的近期和未来融
资，可能对资本市场深度构成挑战。
Ben Connard ， Carnegie Investment Counsel ⾸席投资官表⽰， “ 每个⼈都在流动性枯竭前为建

设计划融资。 ” 他称，最终可能没有⾜够资⾦⽀持整个 AI 扩张，融资可能成为拖慢建设的关键瓶
颈。

### Page 3

财报雪上加霜：现⾦流成为市场新考题
如果债市是预警，财报季则让市场重新评估 AI 投资的回报周期。
⼤型 AI ⽀出⽅的云业务仍在增⻓，但投资者更关注数据中⼼建设成本对⾃由现⾦流的侵蚀。
Anthony Saglimbene ， Ameriprise ⾸席市场策略师表⽰，市场正在变得更有选择性，因为并⾮
每家超⼤规模云⼚商都会成为赢家，关键在于公司花了多少钱，获得多少回报，以及护城河有
多深。
Alphabet 成为最明显的压⼒点。公司财报显⽰核⼼搜索业务销售令⼈失望，且连续第⼆个季度
录得负⾃由现⾦流，这是其上市以来从未出现过的情况。搜索去年占 Alphabet 收⼊近 56% ，因
此 AI 搜索增⻓若伴随更低变现能⼒，将直接触及其核⼼商业模式。该股周四暴跌 13% ，公司市值
蒸发约 5000 亿美元。
Amazon 的报告同样未能安抚市场。由于资本开⽀上升且电商业务增速为三年来最慢， Amazon
负⾃由现⾦流显著差于分析师预期，股价周四和周五累计下跌 5.6% 。
Meta 的压⼒来⾃资本开⽀预期。公司 10 ⽉ 21 ⽇财报显⽰全年资本开⽀计划明显上升，此后股价

下跌 12% 。其信⽤⻛险指标升⾄纪录⾼位。 S&P Global Ratings 周五晚间将 Meta 评级展望从稳
定下调⾄负⾯，称尚不清楚 Meta ⽀出何时能带来超过成本增⻓的收⼊。
现⾦流分化正在成为估值分⽔岭。四家超⼤规模云⼚商中， Amazon ， Meta 和 Alphabet 当季均出
现⾃由现⾦流⾚字。 Amazon 负⾃由现⾦流接近 350 亿美元，⽽⼀年前为正 4.3 亿美元。 Microsoft
是例外，录得 186 亿美元正⾃由现⾦流，但其股价周五仍下跌 1.6% 。
软件板块也被拖⼊ AI 定价重估
AI 焦虑并不局限于巨头和基础设施公司。软件股同样受到冲击。
本财报季 S&P 500 软件公司中， 90% 利润超过预期，但只有 70% 收⼊超过预期，低于上⼀季
度。市场担⼼ Anthropic ， OpenAI 等公司的 AI 产品可能削弱传统软件公司的定价能⼒和利润率。
ServiceNow 发布令⼈失望的季度销售展望后，股价周四暴跌 31% ，创纪录最⼤跌幅。 Fortinet 下

调服务销售预期，引发⽹络安全股抛售。 Twilio 股价周五下跌 18% ，因其展望显⽰增⻓可能明显
放缓。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
Ben Connard 表⽰，市场对哪些软件公司能够在 AI 世界中⽣存 “ 完全不安 ” 。
中期选举临近：政治⻛险进⼊融资模型
第三重压⼒来⾃华盛顿。
Meta 在准备约 980 亿美元股票发⾏之际，在⽂件中提到贸易争端、监管挑战和 “ 公众情绪转变 ” 。
公司警告，新的税收或监管可能进⼀步增加资本⽀出和运营成本。 Anthropic 等公司的⽂件中也
出现类似⻛险披露。
特朗普政府迄今维持对 AI 较宽松的监管态度，强调⾃愿安全措施、公司主导测试和第三⽅审计，
并推动将 AI 重新包装为 “Super Intelligence”。特朗普还在⽩宫接待近⼆⼗名 AI ⾼管，包括
Anthropic ⾸席执⾏官 Dario Amodei ， OpenAI 总裁 Greg Brockman 和 Nvidia ⾸席执⾏官 Jensen

Huang ，并促成⼀项⾮约束性安全承诺。
但公众反弹正在扩⼤。 Washington Post-ABC News-Ipsos 本⽉发布的⺠调显⽰，约三分之⼆美
国⼈反对新建数据中⼼， 57% 的成年⼈认为 AI 对社会是坏事，约 67% 不认可特朗普处理 AI 问题的
⽅式。数据中⼼被视为推⾼电价的重要因素之⼀，也引发环境和劳动⼒冲击担忧。
如果⺠主党在中期选举后夺回国会两院， AI ⾏业可能⾯临更强监管。彭博报道， Hakeem
Jeffries 正在考虑是否成⽴专⻔ AI 委员会，该委员会可能调查并传唤相关公司，重点包括 AI 驱动
的⽹络攻击扩散，以及数据中⼼建设对电价的影响。
政策提案范围也在扩⼤。部分⺠主党⼈⽀持对前沿 AI 模型建⽴联邦监管框架，并预先排除部分州
级规则。两党参议员也在谈判 AI 安全框架。 Bernie Sanders 提出过全国性数据中⼼建设暂停，
Ted Lieu 则提出在模型中设置应对灾难性伤害的关闭机制。
投资者的主线：从 “ 建得快 ” 转向 “ 赚得到 ”

AI 需求仍然强劲，资本开⽀也仍在推进。 Meta 未来⽀出承诺升⾄ 8250 亿美元， Alphabet 未来⽀
出义务超过 1 万亿美元。 Microsoft 称，尽管其财年第⼀季度资本开⽀因瓶颈低于预期，但本财年
下半年⽀出将更⾼。
问题在于，市场不再只奖励⽀出规模。
Zacks Investment Management ⾸席市场策略师 Brian Mulberry 表⽰，投资者正在⽤资⾦投票，
重新定义 “ 质量 ” 。 Fifth Third Wealth Advisors ⾸席投资官 Chris Osmond 称，市场开始奖励能把
⽀出转化为收⼊和实际投资回报的公司，惩罚那些尚未证明这⼀点却继续增加⽀出的公司。
这意味着 AI 交易进⼊ “ 证明阶段 ” 。债市要求更⾼收益率和更强保护条款，股市要求更清晰现⾦流
路径，政治体系则可能要求更严格监管。对投资者⽽⾔， AI 主题没有消失，但筛选标准已经改
变：融资能⼒、⾃由现⾦流、资本开⽀纪律和监管暴露，正在取代单纯的增⻓叙事。

### Page 2

ASIA MONTHLY REPORT  October 2026, No.307

The Japan Research Institute, Limited  
Economics Department

1 
Topics  Loss‑making firms are on the rise in China's growth sectors

In China, the number of companies operating in the red is increasing. And the rise is particularly striking in growth 
sectors. One factor behind the increase is the intense competition among firms, which has led to a situation in which 
only the strongest thrive. Another is that many next‑generation growth companies, despite being viewed as having great 
potential, are losing money.

■ Loss‑making firms are on the rise in growth sectors 
In recent years, the number of companies in China's 
growth sectors that are operating in the red has 
increased markedly. The share of loss-making firms in 
all listed firms (below, "loss‑making‑firm ratio") rose 
sharply from 9.6% in 2019 to 26.9% in 2025. Looking 
at the industry distribution of loss‑making firms, from 
2019 to 2022, the ratio increased mainly in 
non‑manufacturing sectors such as real estate and 
accommodation and eating/drinking services, 
reflecting the property market downturn and strict 
stay‑at‑home measures implemented to curb the spread 
of COVID‑19. Later, from 2022 to 2025, the 
loss‑making-firm ratio surged in 
information/communications equipment and 
electronic parts/devices; general, production, and 
commercial machinery; electrical machine ry; and 
chemicals. These four sectors alone accounted for 
around 60% of the overall increase. 
They are the principal growth sectors in Chinese 
manufacturing. This is evidenced by the fact that the 
growth rates of value added in all four sectors exceed 
the average for Chinese manufacturing. Many of the 
high‑tech manufacturing industries that the Chi nese 
government has prioritized for development are 
included in these sectors. For example, 5G base 
stations and semiconductors fall under 
information/communications equipment and 
electronic parts/devices. And robots fall under general, 
production, and com mercial machinery. Meanwhile, 
solar cells and batteries fall under electrical machinery, 
and biopharmaceuticals and organic chemical 
materials fall under chemicals.  
Looking at recent developments in the 
manufacturing of information/communications 
equipment and electronic parts/devices, the global 
communications standard began shifting from 4G 
(LTE) to 5G services in around 2019, and ChatGPT was released in November 2022. These developments 
have provided tailwinds for the growth of the sector. Recently, as it has become clear that ordinary 
consumers and companies can make use of AI services on a daily basis, demand for AI‑related devices and 
semiconductors, as well as d ata centers, has soared. In this environment, many firms are focusing on 
strengthening development, manufacturing, and sales, and the sector's value added has continued to 
increase, rising from 780 billion yuan in 2022 to 910 billion yuan in 2025. Yet over  the same period, the 
number of loss‑making firms increased from 113 to 198, and the loss‑making‑firm ratio jumped from 17.2% 
to 30.1%. 
■ Competition among companies means only the strongest thrive 
One reason loss‑making firms are increasing despite operating in growth sectors is the intense 
competition among companies. The fight for market share has led to overheated investment competition

2 
and price competition. In information/communications 
equipment and electronic parts/devices manufacturing, 
the rate of growth of non -current assets from 2022 to 
2025 (+35.4%) far exceeded the growth rate of value 
added (+16.9%). This indicates that supply capacity is 
expanding faster than demand. In addition, the 
consumer price index (CPI) increased by just 0.4% 
over the same period. Service prices rose 2.2%, 
reflecting wage increases, while goods prices fell 0.7% 
due to excess supply in manufacturing. 
Information/communications equipment prices 
decreased by 3.5%, larger than the drop for goods 
overall. 
As the selection of the fittest through competition 
among companies has progressed, profits have become 
concentrated at a small number of highly competitive 
firms. The share of profits earned by the top 1% of 
firms in information/communications equipment and electronic parts/devices manufacturing rose sharply 
from 24.0% in 2022 to 43.3% in 2025. Leading companies are simultaneously establishing positions as 
both "domestic champions" and "global champions."  
■ Next‑generation growth companies are viewed as having great potential even though they 
are losing money 
However, loss‑making firms cannot simply be lumped together and labeled as losers. Globally, investors 
have shown increasing interest recently in companies with high growth potential even if they are operating 
in the red, and China has a considerable number of such firms. 
In information/communications equipment and 
electronic parts/devices manufacturing, one out of 
every two loss‑making firms is a company attracting 
strong investor interest. The Shanghai Composite 
Index rose 28.5% from 3,089 points at the beginning of 
2023 to 3,969 points at the end of 2025. 20 of the 198 
loss‑making firms in 2025 were not yet listed in 2023, 
and if these are excluded, there were 178 companies 
operating in the red as of 2025. Of these 178 firms, 96 
(53.9%) saw stock‑price increases over the period 
2023-2025 that exceeded the rise in the Shanghai 
Composite Index.  
One characteristic of these 96 companies with strong 
relative stock‑price performance is their active 
investment in R&D and intangible assets. Their 
average R&D intensity (R&D investment / sales) in 
2022 was 9.6%, and their ratio of intangible assets to 
value added stood at 60.5%. Intangible assets include 
computer software, patents, copyrights, and 
relationships with customers and suppliers. In contrast, 
the 82 loss‑making firms with weak relative 
stock‑price performance had an R&D intensity of only 
6.5% and an intangible‑asset‑to‑value‑added ratio of just 38.9%. 
As the above has shown, the increase in loss‑making firms in China's growth sectors is notable, and the 
factors behind it include not only the selection of the fittest as a result of competition among companies but 
also the emergence of next‑generation growth firms that are viewed as having great potential. 
  (Shinichi Seki)

Topics  Climate change heightens the risk of growth stagnation in Asia

Climate change could elevate the risk of economic growth flatlining in the ASEAN zone and India by causing labor 
productivity growth and capital accumulation to stall. To mitigate or avert such a scenario, establishing a business 
environment and institutions that promote "adaptation investment" by both the public and private sectors is an urgent 
task.

■ Heavy‑rain and flood damage is becoming increasingly serious in Asia 
Huge downpours and floods 
resulting in human casualties have 
been a frequent occurrence in Asia in 
the past several years. Still fresh in the 
memory is the debris flow that 
occurred in August this year near the 
border between Nepal and China, 
which as of t he time of writing has 
been recognized as a major disaster 
with thousands dead or missing. And 
in November of last year, Southeast 
Asia and South Asia saw 
record‑breaking torrential rains, 
resulting in numerous casualties, 
particularly in Indonesia and Sri  
Lanka. 
One factor that heightens the risks 
of such floods and heavy rains is 
climate change. As temperatures rise, 
the amount of water vapor in the 
atmosphere increases. Therefore, 
global warming could intensify 
torrential rainfall. Although such risk 
is shared a cross Asia, the way its 
effects are felt differs by region. 
Within Asia, the ASEAN zone and 
India are heavily affected by 
monsoons (seasonal winds), with 
many areas experiencing concentrated 
rainfall during the wet season. In such 
areas, warming can furthe r intensify 
monsoon‑driven heavy rains, 
increasing the risks of floods and 
landslides.  
■ Climate change will delay the escape from the "middle‑income trap" 
Climate change not only results in human casualties by way of natural disasters, but may also undermine 
the long‑term economic growth potential of ASEAN and India through various channels. The Asian 
Development Bank (ADB) [2024] conducted a quantitative an alysis of the extent to which climate change 
will weigh on Asian economies over the long term. Based on the IPCC's "very high GHG emissions" 
scenario (SSP5‑8.5), the analysis assumes a 4.7°C rise in the Earth's average temperature by 2100 relative 
to pre-industrial levels, and estimates the difference in real GDP between that and a hypothetical scenario 
without climate change. The results illustrate the long‑term impact on economic growth through declines 
in labor productivity, stagnation in capital accumulation, and downw ard pressure on consumption due to 
sea‑level rise, floods, heat waves, and other phenomena caused by climate change.  
An examination of the results reveals that in every country/territory, the pushdown effect on real GDP 
relative to the no‑climate‑change scenario expands over time, with 2035, 2050, and 2070 each seeing 
greater impact. The pushdown effect for ASEAN and Ind ia is −15.0% to −8.8% in 2050 and −30.2% to

−18.1% in 2070, larger than the effects for China (2050: −4.8%, 2070: −10.3%) and Central Asian countries 
(2050: −1.7%, 2070: −3.8%). Within Asia, the ASEAN zone and India can be considered regions where the 
risk of economic stagnation due to climate change is relatively large.  
This may make it even more difficult for ASEAN countries and India to escape the "middle‑income trap." 
Although their income levels have risen, they still remain far from high‑income status. Indonesia, Malaysia, 
the Philippines, and Thailand became middle‑income countries in the 1990s or earlier, but have still not 
been able to join the groups of high‑income countries. Furthermore, Vietnam and India, which became 
middle‑income countries in the 2000s or later, have yet to enter a high‑growth trajectory like China once 
did. Climate change, by causing labor productivity growth to stall amid rising temperatures and companies 
to curtail investment due to more frequent disasters, may lead to income growth in the ASEAN zone and 
India being delayed further.  
■ Improving the environment to accelerate "adaptation investment" will be important 
To alleviate the pushdown effect of climate change on growth, expansion of "adaptation investment" by 
the public and private sectors is essential. Specifically, this could include improving working environments 
and promoting measures to lighten workloads a nd reduce headcounts in order to offset declines in labor 
productivity caused by rising temperatures, as well as developing infrastructure and strengthening facilities 
to prevent damage to production equipment from sea‑level rise and floods. However, ASEAN  and India 
cannot be said to have sufficiently 
developed the foundations for public 
and private promotion of such 
adaptation investment.  
As part of its Global Adaptation 
Initiative (ND‑GAIN), the University of 
Notre Dame (U.S.) evaluates countries' 
ability to use investment to achieve 
climate‑change adaptation in the form of 
a "readiness" score with three 
components: economic readiness, 
governance readiness, and social 
readiness. Respectively, these 
components assess 1) whether a 
business environment conducive to 
adaptation investment by private -sector 
companies has been put in place, 2) 
whether the effectiveness of institutions

and policies to support private-sector adaptation investment has been secured, and 3) whether tangible and 
intangible social infrastructure that promotes investment has been established. 
Looking at readiness in 2024, ASEAN countries and India generally rank in the middle to lower tiers 
globally, with a large gap between them and developed countries. Notably, the Philippines, India, and 
Thailand fall outside the top 100, indicating weak momentum for adaptation investment. Breaking down 
the scores, problems are especially evident in the economic and governance categories. India and Thailand 
have particularly low eco nomic readiness, ranking 113th and 124th, respectively, and five countries 
(Malaysia not included) fall outside the top 100 for governance readiness. Given this situation, to accelerate 
private‑sector adaptation investment, India and Thailand in particular  need to move swiftly to improve the 
financial environment to make it easier to procure necessary funds domestically and internationally, relax 
regulations to encourage the launch of new businesses, and reform labor markets to enable the smooth 
securing of human resources. In addition, for Asia as a whole, it will also be important to boost confidence 
in the institutional factors that enhance application of investment for adaptation by increasing policy 
stability, ensuring the effectiveness of legal systems, and curbing corruption.

(Tomohiro Hosoi)

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Dầu khí, Bảo hiểm và Dịch vụ phân phối là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -3,013 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã MCH, VIC và FPT; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng ở mức 
887 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -2,021 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -4,384 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng -
4,275 hợp đồng và đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -7,815 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam tiếp tục điều chỉnh tuần thứ tư liên tiếp, khi VN-Index lùi về đóng cửa 1.706 
điểm - giảm khoảng 1,7% so với tuần trước và đang dao động quanh vùng hỗ trợ tâm lý 
1.700 điểm, cũng là vùng đáy cũ của tháng 8. Thanh khoản thị trường tiếp tục suy yếu, 
phản ánh tâm lý thận trọng cao độ trong bối cảnh thị trường thiếu vắng thông tin hỗ trợ 
trong nước, trong khi các yếu tố bên ngoài như xung đột tại Iran, và chi phí vốn toàn cầu 
chưa hạ nhiệt tiếp tục đè nặng tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Khối ngoại duy trì chuỗi bán ròng kéo 
dài nhiều phiên liên tiếp, nối dài đà rút vốn đã lên tới xấp xỉ 95 nghìn tỷ đồng từ đầu năm. 
Trong một, hai tuần tới, mùa báo cáo kết quả kinh doanh quý 3 được kỳ vọng là chất xúc 
tác giúp thị trường giao dịch cân bằng trở lại.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tiếp tục hình thành mẫu nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên đồ thị tuần, đánh dấu tuần 
giảm thứ 4 liên tiếp, cho thấy áp lực phân phối vẫn đang có phần lấn át. Trên khung ngày, 
tuy chỉ số đã có nỗ lực phục hồi trở lại trong 3 phiên đan xen, đặc biệt là phản ứng trong 
phiên cuối tuần khi chạm trendline trung hạn, nhưng việc chỉ có thể hình thành các mẫu 
nến "spinning" hàm ý đà tích lũy chưa thực sự thuyết phục. Trong một vài phiên kế tiếp, 
VNINDEX có thể xuất hiện nhịp hồi kỹ thuật từ vùng hỗ trợ, tương ứng với cạnh dưới tam 
giác, nhưng sau khi chớm phá vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1710 và hiện chỉ còn một số ít các mã giữ 
được xu hướng tăng tính từ đáy tháng 7, xu hướng ngắn hạn đang trở nên khá trung tính.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên)

- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng.

- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua.

### Page 2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX tăng điểm trong tuần qua, +68.24 điểm tương ứng +4.00%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Thiết bị điện, Khách sạn - Nhà hàng và Thiết bị dịch vụ y tế là các nhóm ngành có 
mức tăng rõ rệt nhất trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần 
trước với giá trị -966 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã FPT, ACB và VCB; mặt khác, tự doanh duy 
trì mua ròng ở mức 137 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều mua ròng 1,335 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua nhưng đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -3,049 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng 
1,815 hợp đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -6,000 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam hồi phục trở lại sau bốn tuần giảm liên tiếp, khi VN-Index bật tăng mạnh 
từ vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1.700 điểm với thanh khoản cải thiện. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh kéo dài 
khiến mặt bằng định giá về vùng hấp dẫn, lực cầu bắt đáy đã nhập cuộc quyết liệt hơn, 
với phiên 21/10 tăng gần 47 điểm — một trong những phiên tăng mạnh nhất nhiều tháng 
gần đây. Điểm tích cực và đáng chú ý nhất là khối ngoại đã xuất hiện hai phiên đảo chiều 
mua ròng trở lại sau khoảng 20 phiên bán ròng liên tiếp, tập trung vào nhóm Bluechip 
như VIC, TCB, VHM, MSN — tín hiệu đầu tiên cho thấy áp lực rút vốn ngoại có thể đang dần 
hạ nhiệt. Thông tin tích cực tác động lên thị trường đến từ các chỉ đạo của NHNN và Chính 
phủ về việc hạ mặt bằng lãi suất để hỗ trợ tăng trưởng kinh tế. Bên cạnh đó, mùa báo cáo 
KQKD quý 3 khả quan đang diễn ra cũng là yếu tố hỗ trợ xu hướng thị trường chung.

19/10/2026

Nguồn: V_KBSource

Triển vọng kinh tế 4Q2026 
Giữ nhịp tăng trưởng, vững vàng về itch

Một số dự báo chính của KBSV về kinh tế Việt Nam trong năm 2026:

1) Tăng trưởng GDP năm 2026 được dự báo sẽ đạt 9.0%, với động lực chính 
đến từ định hướng đẩy mạnh giải ngân đầu tư công của Chính phủ, dòng 
vốn FDI duy trì tích cực và hoạt động sản xuất công nghiệp phục hồi theo 
xuất khẩu. Về rủi ro, chúng tôi cho rằng Việt Nam sẽ tiếp tục đối mặt với 
một số yếu tố bất định từ bên ngoài, bao gồm: rủi ro xung đột Mỹ – Iran 
kéo dài hơn dự kiến, xu hướng tăng của mặt bằng lãi suất toàn cầu gây gia 
tăng áp lực tài chính và kéo giảm tổng cầu, cùng nguy cơ vỡ bong bóng AI.

2) Chúng tôi nâng dự báo lạm phát bình quân năm 2026 của Việt Nam lên 
mức 4.7% YoY, cao hơn mục tiêu kiểm soát lạm phát của Chính phủ, dựa 
trên kịch bản dự báo thận trọng hơn về diễn biến xung đột địa chính trị và 
giá năng lượng thế giới trong ngắn hạn.

3) Mặt bằng lãi suất trong quý IV được dự báo sẽ ổn định quanh vùng hiện 
tại và không xuất hiện thêm nhịp tăng đột biến, nhờ dòng tiền cải thiện 
từ giải ngân đầu tư công và kỳ vọng có thêm các biện pháp hỗ trợ thanh 
khoản trung dài hạn từ phía Chính phủ và NHNN.

4) Tỷ giá USD/VND trong quý cuối năm có thể chịu một số sức ép do Fed dự 
báo còn thêm một lần tăng lãi suất nữa trong năm. Dù vậy, chúng tôi cho 
rằng tỷ giá USD/VND khó tăng mạnh mà chỉ quay lại mức đầu năm, dựa 
trên kỳ vọng chỉ số DXY không biến động mạnh, nguồn cung ngoại tệ cải 
thiện (FDI giải ngân, kiều hối, nâng hạng TTCK) và chênh lệch lãi suất 
VND–USD duy trì trạng thái dương.

Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

### Page 4

4

21/10/2026

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025. 
— PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ. 
Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành cây nến xanh thân dài trên khung tuần và đáng chú ý là cho phản ứng 
bật tăng ngay tại cạnh dưới của mẫu hình tam giác đi ngang trung hạn, cho thấy tín hiệu 
tích cực đã quay trở lại với xu hướng ngắn hạn. Trên khung ngày, sau 2 phiên điều chỉnh, 
rung lắc vào đầu tuần, chỉ số cũng đảo chiều tăng điểm khá dốc, với 3 cây nến xanh liên 
tiếp với thanh khoản được tăng cường, củng cố thêm tín hiệu xác nhận cho đà hồi phục. 
Sau nhịp bật tăng mạnh từ đáy, VNINDEX được dự báo sẽ sớm gặp phải áp lực điều chỉnh 
khi quay lên điểm giữa của dải đi ngang theo mẫu hình tam giác, tương ứng vùng kháng cự 
kế tiếp tại quanh 1810. Tuy nhiên, xu hướng ngắn hạn vẫn có phần nghiêng về chiều tăng, 
với tín hiệu xác nhận đáy của một số mã cổ phiếu sau quá trình sụt giảm kéo dài.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên)

trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua.

## Document 287: CPO相關設備趨勢與台廠投資機會 福邦投顧研究部202610

Source PDF: all report pdf/CPO相關設備趨勢與台廠投資機會 福邦投顧研究部202610.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-24
Pages: 30
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

CPO相關設備趨勢與台廠投資機會
福邦投顧研究部
2026/10

### Page 2

2【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
結論
◼ 需求強勁
CPO 自 2027 年進入放量期，光引擎需求 2026-2028 年由 62 萬顆增至 3,500 萬顆：AI 瓶頸由算力轉向傳輸，
Pluggable 於 3.2T 世代碰到功耗與密度上限，Scale-out 交換機率先轉向 CPO，2028 年起由 Rubin Ultra NVL576
跨櫃 Scale-up 接棒。OE 占 CPO 交換機 BOM 約四成，為價值鏈核心。
◼ 關鍵瓶頸
瓶頸測試與耦合為量產瓶頸，2028 年測試設備 TAM 約 418 億元、逾七成在 Ins3/Ins4：每顆 OE 須以次微米精
度主動對光，且模組良率隨 OE 數量倍數下滑，每一關都必須篩出良品，使 OE 成本近六成落在後段。Ins2 若
跳過，2027/2028 年整體 TAM 縮減 12%/16%，但 Ins3 測試機與貼合機需求反增。
◼ 台廠機會
台廠以致茂、鴻勁最確定，萬潤受惠貼合，其餘仍屬驗證期選擇權：致茂的 Ins3 OE 測試機已取得 2027 年量產

訂單，加上 Ins4 光學測試，預估 2027 年 CPO 營收 65-75 億元；鴻勁以光電同測 Handler 切入 Ins4，預估 55-60
億元；萬潤是 FAU/receptacle 耦合貼合的主要供應商，若採 receptacle 兩段貼合，貼合機需求最高可倍增。漢測
、旺矽、光焱仍在驗證階段，能否放量取決於 Ins2 的去留與台積電 HVM 驗證結果。
◼ 看好個股
致茂 (2360)、鴻勁 (7769)、萬潤 (6187)

AI 傳輸瓶頸推動 CPO 導入，2027 年進入放量期

### Page 5

5【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
CSP資本支出今明年延續高成長，支出重心可能逐步轉向網通設備
資料來源：BBG；福邦投顧整理
2026-27年CSP資本支出續強 ，主權AI投資有望接棒支撐 2028年成長：五大CSP資本支出於2026年年增約98%，
2027年仍有望成長47%，惟隨基期墊高，2028年年增率預估放緩至11%。但觀察到近期中東、歐洲等地主權AI計
畫與Neo cloud陸續宣布更多的投資及合作 ，中國亦積極布局AI資料中心，有望拓展CSP以外的需求來源，延續
整體AI基礎建設投資動能。
記憶體成本攀升 ，AI資本支出有望逐步向網通設備傾斜： 2025年資料中心成本中 ，IT設備約占整體支出 84%，
其中伺服器約占IT設備8成，網通與存儲設備則占其餘部分 。隨記憶體價格上漲、BOM成本攀升，NVIDIA已開
始調整Rubin Ultra的HBM規格，以優化整體成本。且若單顆GPU記憶體容量受限，MoE模型參數分配、Prefill /
Decode分離及KV Cache跨節點搬移等需求，可能推升GPU間的資料傳輸量，推升高速網路的重要性。

### Page 6

6【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
AI 算 力 成 長 超 越 資 料 傳 輸 能 力， 高 速 互 聯 成 為 系 統 效 能 關 鍵
資料來源：AI and Memory Wall；Nvidia；福邦投顧整理
AI晶片算力成長快於記憶體頻寬與互聯傳輸 ，系統瓶頸由「算不夠快」轉向「資料送不夠快」： 隨前沿模型參
數規模超越單顆GPU記憶體容量，須透過張量平行等方式拆分至多顆GPU協同運算，增加跨GPU資料交換需求。
根據學界統計，過去20年硬體峰值算力約每 2年成長3倍，DRAM頻寬與互聯頻寬卻僅成長 1.6倍及1.4倍，差距持
續擴大，使GPU等待資料的時間增加，形成「I/O Wall」。
推論需求由MoE延伸至超長上下文 ，任務碎片化與資料搬移推升互聯頻寬需求： MoE專家數量增至300個以上，
加上推論任務更加碎片化，需透過GPU間all-to-all通訊，進而推升Scale-up頻寬需求。進入長上下文階段後，影音
生成與軟體開發等應用的上下文長度突破100萬個token，帶動KV cache大幅增長；在GPU記憶體容量有限及Prefill

/ Decode分離部署下，資料須跨GPU與節點搬移，將推升Scale-out頻寬需求。隨模型複雜度與資料傳輸需求持續
提升，高速互聯成為避免GPU閒置、支撐AI效能成長的關鍵，也為網通設備投資提供長期動能。
圖3、I/O牆逐步成為算力瓶頸 圖4、推論複雜度由Dense演進至MoE與超長上下文

### Page 7

7【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
Scale-out Switch逐漸轉向CPO，NVIDIA、Broadcom產品2027年放量
資料來源：SemiAnalysis；Broadcom；福邦投顧整理
功耗與密度雙重瓶頸，推動Scale-out交換機由Pluggable轉向CPO： 在功耗方面，Pluggable鏈路需經PCB走線
與DSP進行訊號傳輸及補償 ，隨頻寬提升，Insertion Loss增加，訊號處理功耗也隨之攀升； CPO將OE直接整
合於Switch ASIC周圍，使訊號離開晶片後即可轉為光，減少訊號處理環節。在密度方面，GPU數量增加帶動
Port需求成長，但Pluggable受限於前面板插槽空間；CPO則將OE配置於ASIC周圍，突破前面板的密度限制。
目前CPO應用以NVIDIA為主、Broadcom其次，Spectrum-X推出有望帶動2027年擴大部署：CPO目前主要應
用於 Scale-out交換機 。NVIDIA轉向開放式乙太網架構後 ，客戶接受度提升 ，已知客戶包括 CoreWeave、

Lambda等Neo cloud及Oracle；隨Spectrum-X於2H26推出，預期2027年將擴大部署 。相較之下 ，Broadcom的
CPO交換機目前以Meta為主要客戶，多數雲端業者仍多採用純電版本，CPO尚未大規模導入。
圖5、CPO與DSP可插拔光模組訊號路徑差異 表1、Nvidia、Broadcom CPO交換機新產品

Rubin Ultra將導入光互聯Scale-up，Feynman可望全面轉向CPO
資料來源：SemiAnalysis；福邦投顧整理
Scale-up轉光最快有望於Rubin Ultra NVL576落地：MoE模型架構的all-to-all通訊推動Scale-up網域由72顆GPU
擴大至576顆，需串聯8個機櫃形成單一NVLink網域。目前機櫃內仍以銅背板互連為主 ，跨機櫃則導入光互聯
，NPO（Socket）與CPO方案均在開發中。Rubin Ultra採用9+9+9的Oberon機櫃設計，中間9個Switch Tray各配
置4顆NVLink Switch ASIC，每顆ASIC搭配4顆3.2T OE，推算每櫃配置144顆OE、每組NVL576共1,152顆。
Feynman NVL1152機櫃間將全面導入CPO：下一代Feynman NVL1152將由8個Kyber機櫃組成，機櫃間同樣採
用CPO；NVIDIA Roadmap亦已明確規劃Feynman將採用NVLink 8，並於機櫃間互聯全面導入CPO，機櫃內則

可能由銅互聯轉為CPO。

9【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
隨CPO / NPO Switch 逐步放量，OE需求有望於2026–2028年快速攀升
資料來源：Nvidia、台積電、廣發、福邦投顧整理預估
台廠Scale-out為2026–2028年CPO主要應用 ，2027年OE需求有望年增逾 18倍：以三層 Fat-Tree架構及 Spectrum
6800/6810為基礎推估，假設NVIDIA於2026/2027/2028年的CPO滲透率分別為10%/30%/50%，Scale-out CPO交換
機出貨量約為 4.8/91.8/154.7k台，對應OE需求約616/11,748/19,800k顆，2027/2028年年增率分別約 1,807%/69%。
預估2026–2027年CPO OE需求將主要來自Scale-out應用。
Scale-up初期以NPO導入，帶動2028年OE總需求上看3,500萬顆： 以Rubin Ultra NVL576每櫃配置144顆OE估算
，並考量良率及維修性需求，假設Scale-up初期採用NPO方案。若2027/2028年Rubin Ultra採用NVL576的比例分

別為 30%/50% ， 對應 Scale-up NPO OE 需求約 720/15,200k 顆 。 綜合 Scale-out CPO 與 Scale-up NPO 需求 ，
2026/2027/2028年OE總需求約為 616/12,468/35,000k顆，2027/2028年年增率分別約 1,924%/181%；其中CPO占比
將由100%降至94.2%及56.6%，顯示隨Scale-up自2028年起放量，NPO OE需求占比將明顯提升。

台積電主導 CPO 封裝，測試與耦合成量產瓶頸

### Page 11

11【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
Scale-out先行CPO，Scale-up因良率可能先採NPO過渡
圖8、從光模組到CPO的封裝工藝路線圖，過渡階段可能先以NPO的形式來做Scale-up
資料來源：TSMC、福邦投顧整理
Scale-out CPO現階段採OE on Substrate封裝：依台積電光互連技術路徑，目前Scale-out CPO交換機主要採OE
on Substrate架構，將OE與Switch ASIC共同封裝，尚未進展至將OE整合於Interposer的OOI階段。待封裝良率改
善後，OE有望進一步移至Interposer，相較現行CPO仍有約2.5倍的能效提升空間，為長期發展方向。
Scale-up初期可能先以Socket式NPO為主，以效能換取良率與維修性：NVL576跨櫃互聯提供CPO與NPO兩種方
案，CPO將OE直接封裝並外接雷射光源，NPO則透過Connector以Socket形式安裝OE，可單獨更換。CPO單一封
裝整合數十顆OE，任一顆失效即可能整顆報廢，良率隨OE數量增加而下降；NPO雖因多經Substrate走線與連接

器而效能略遜，但可避開此風險。

12【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
台積電COUPE拿下NVIDIA與Broadcom，CPO平台領先同業
圖9、各家矽光、封裝平台整理
資料來源：台積電、穎崴、福邦投顧整理
台積電以SoIC-X混合鍵合拿下NVIDIA與Broadcom兩大CPO客戶：SoIC-X將EIC直接鍵合於PIC上方，大幅降低
寄生效應，使NVIDIA得以實現200G/λ MRM，平台亦同時支援Broadcom的MZM路線。加上GC耦合支援晶圓級測
試與量產、完整PDK元件庫，以及由CPO延伸至OOI的路線圖，形成其他平台短期難以複製的整體方案 ，為目前
最佳的CPO量產平台。
新材料世代仍為其他平台留下切入機會：Broadcom陣營同步採用GF與Tower分散供應風險；若400G/λ世代須轉向
TFLN或InP等新材料，平台優勢可能重新洗牌。

### Page 13

13【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
台積電COUPE兼具GC與EC耦合，兼顧量產測試與低損耗
圖10、台積電COUPE-GC與COUPE-EC耦合架構
資料來源：台積電、福邦投顧整理
GC垂直耦合可做晶圓級測試 ，為COUPE率先量產的方案：COUPE-GC在PIC上製作光柵耦合器 ，光訊號垂直進
出，經Si Carrier上的Si lens聚焦並擴大對位公差，下方金屬反射層（MR）再回收散失的光以降低損耗。由於光從
晶圓表面進出，切割前即可逐顆量測，可直接銜接Insertion 1晶圓級測試。
EC邊緣耦合損耗更低，但須切割後才能對光：COUPE-EC由晶片側邊Facet進光，以SiN Tip轉換光模態進入波導
，損耗較低且對波長 、偏振較不敏感，利於往更高頻寬與 DWDM發展；惟須切割後才能耦光 ，Facet品質與對位
精度要求更高，測試須延後至Insertion 3。

### Page 14

14【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
光引擎佔CPO交換機價值40–50%，為最高價值零組件
圖11、CPO交換機價值鏈：OE 佔整機價值 40–50%
資料來源：GS、福邦投顧整理
OE為CPO交換機中價值最高的零組件 ，約占BOM成本4成：根據GS預估CPO交換機成本拆解來看，以NVIDIA
Quantum-X 交換機為例，總BOM成本約7.58萬美元，其中72顆光引擎合計約3.24萬美元，占BOM約43%，為4顆
Switch ASIC（占16%）的2.7倍；顯示CPO交換機的價值核心已由交換晶片轉向光學系統 。OE由PIC與EIC經
SoIC混合鍵合而成，負責電光轉換、調變與驅動，取代傳統可插拔光模組的核心功能；製程上須歷經 PIC與EIC
晶圓製造、混合鍵合、切割與receptacle耦光貼合，期間並穿插多道晶圓級與晶粒級光電測試 ，製程複雜度與附
加價值皆集中於OE。

### Page 15

15【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
OE成本近6成在封測，測試佔SiPh元件成本75%
圖12、OE成本結構、與SiPh元件製造成本拆解
資料來源：智璞、福邦投顧整理
OE成本近6成來自封裝與測試，晶圓製造僅約4成：以單顆OE約100美元拆解，PIC、EIC晶圓與SoIC堆疊
分別約占11%、17%、14%，合計約42%，其餘59%來自封裝與測試；若PIC或耦光良率偏低，報廢晶片將
推升晶圓端占比。
SiPh測試成本占比約75%，遠高於InP元件的29%：每顆die須先完成光纖對位才能量測，加上PDK未成熟
、多在切單後逐顆測試；若在晶圓階段完成KGD測試，占比可由封裝後的90%降至約60%，具晶圓級光測
與高精度對位能力的設備廠最受惠。

### Page 16

16【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
CPO封裝過程需經4道主要測試，以決定CPO量產良率
圖13、CPO光引擎4道測試流程與設備供應商
資料來源：富果、TrendForce、MoneyDJ、福邦投顧整理
CPO需額外加入光學測試，測試複雜度大幅提高：光電混合元件除電性外，還須驗證光訊號，凡涉及PIC的
測試都須先將光精準耦合進晶片，且OE封裝後難以重工，因此須在晶圓、Hybrid bonding、OE切割及模組組
裝完成等節點確保每顆OE皆為良品，由於光耦合對位耗時，量產瓶頸正由「對得準」轉向「測得快」。
CPO良率會隨OE數量呈倍數下滑： 一顆Switch ASIC周圍需搭載30顆以上的OE，假設單顆OE良率為95%，
以32顆計算，整顆CPO模組良率僅約19%；若單顆OE良率提升至99%，模組良率才能回升至約72%。任一顆
OE失效都可能導致整組模組報廢，因此必須在每道製程前先篩除不良品。
Insertion 1 Insertion 2 Insertion 3 Insertion 4

測試重點 分別對PIC、EIC兩種晶圓做wafer-level 測試 EIC Hybrid bonding在PIC上後，以上電下光做雙面wafer-level測試 切割後完整 OE (EIC + PIC + FAU) 測試；KGOE (Known Good OE) 確認光迴路 完整 CPO Module + Switch ASIC +多 OE系統測試整機鏈路驗證

17【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
Insertion 1：量產測試確定導入，測試時間有望大幅縮減
資料來源：Advantest、致茂、光焱、汎銓、TrendForce 、福邦投顧預估整理
PIC耦光對位為測試瓶頸 ，漏光預篩可望成為提速關鍵： Ins1於晶圓階段對EIC與PIC進行CP測試，EIC沿用一
般電性Probing，PIC則需在Prober加裝光學對位模組，量測光路損耗與MRM、PD等元件特性。由於每顆die都
須以次微米精度重新對準光纖，測試耗時成為量產瓶頸。業界正導入漏光定位設備作為預篩，先剔除漏光或有
潛在缺陷的die，免跑完整量測，以縮短整片晶圓的總測試時間 ；其中光焱NightJar 單格掃描不到1秒，量產版
NightJar-H正於台積電進行HVM驗證。
台廠方面：光焱可望受惠於預篩設備導入，致茂則以PIC整機方案送驗中，若通過驗證可望切入Insertion 1。
圖14、PIC檢測機台示意圖 圖15、PIC/EIC晶圓測試重點整理
項目
測試內容 EIC：同一般電性ProbingPIC：Prober加裝光學對位模組，打光量測損耗

核心目的 檢測光路損耗（耦合器、波導）及MRM、濾波元件、PD等元件特性
核心技術 6軸平台夾持光纖探針、可調雷射＋光功率計、波長掃描找微環共振；選配紅外線漏光定位
主要瓶頸 每顆die需重新對準光纖，次微米級閉環耦合，耗時過長
關鍵供應鏈 測試機：Teradyne、Advantest；探針台/卡：TEL、FormFactor；6軸平台：PI；光學量測：Keysight
市場現況 先以漏光定位篩出潛在缺陷die，PIC測試由100秒/die縮至<5秒/die
台廠受惠 致茂（整機方案驗證中）、光焱（NightJar-H於台積電HVM驗證）、汎銓（研發除錯）

### Page 18

18【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
Insertion 2：技術尚未成熟仍待定，去留成觀察重點
資料來源：Advantest、Teradyne、旺矽、致茂、光焱、漢測、TrendForce ；福邦投顧整理
Ins2為CPO測試最難一站，雙面架構拖慢產出：Ins2於EIC以SoIC混合鍵合至PIC晶圓後、切割前進行測試，以上
電下光方式量測光迴路 、電光轉換之間的各種參數 。由於測試價值隨階段往後遞增 ，提前剔除不良die可提升後
續封裝良率，並劃分晶圓廠與OSAT之間的良率責任。此站需一面電性接觸、一面光耦合並同步精準對位 ，並兼
顧EIC發熱對Micro-Ring的影響 ，雙面架構難以多site平行測試，現行方案多為單site，單片晶圓測試約需6小時以
上，因耗時過長部分業者傾向暫時跳過，2027年放量看法分歧。
台廠方面：Advantest + 旺矽持續出二代機驗證中，漢測主要幫ficonTEC代工，光焱則提供快篩替代方案。
圖16、EIC/PIC D2W晶粒示意圖 圖17、EIC+PIC鍵合後晶圓測試重點整理

項目
測試內容 EIC以SoIC鍵合至PIC晶圓後、切割前，上電下光測E/O、O/E、O/O、光迴路及S參數
核心目的 測試價值隨階段遞增，提前剔除不良die以提升後續封裝良率
核心技術 雙面探測（一面電性接觸、一面光耦合）並同步對位；晶粒級溫控，避免EIC發熱影響微環共振
主要瓶頸 難以多site平行測試，多為單site、產出低；光耦合易受震動、溫度與翹曲影響
關鍵供應鏈 Teradyne＋ficonTEC（第二版驗證中）；Advantest＋旺矽（工程機已出貨，量產驗證中）
市場現況 單片晶圓測試約6小時以上；部分業者傾向暫時跳過，2027年放量看法分歧
台廠受惠 旺矽（Prober＋Probe card）、漢測（ficonTEC代工）、光焱（快篩替代方案）

### Page 19

19【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
Insertion 3：Die Level全檢不可省，Shift to middle成CPO測試長期主力
資料來源：致茂、穎崴、萬潤、漢測、TrendForce；福邦投顧整理
Insertion 3為Die Level全檢，單顆取放拉長測試時間： Insertion 3於OE切割並貼合receptacle後進行測試，量測
光路品質（IL、PDL）與loopback誤碼率（BER），目的是避免切割、貼合過程產生的不良OE流入後續載板黏
合製程。相較Insertion 2的Wafer Level測試，Insertion 3須單顆取放、溫控與對接並全數檢測，未來可能每顆OE
的測試時間約為Insertion 2 per die的2–3倍。量產前全檢為必要條件，部分流程已導入大功率與老化測試，測試
時間仍有上升空間，但未來可能往多site平行測試發展以提升產出。
台廠方面：漢測（ficonTEC驗證中）、致茂(Tester出量產機)，萬潤則為FAU / receptacle耦合貼合主要供應商。

圖18、CPO光電訊號路徑示意圖 圖19、切割後OE（KGOE）測試重點整理
項目
測試內容 切割後OE貼合receptacle後測試
核心目的 避免不良OE流入載板黏合製程
核心技術 光路品質（IL、PDL）、loopback BER
主要瓶頸 單顆取放＋全檢，測試時間拉長1–2倍
關鍵供應鏈 FiconTEC；致茂、穎崴（Socket）

20【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
Insertion 4：Switch ASIC+OE成品測試，SLT為最後一塊拼圖
資料來源：台積電、鴻勁、致茂、TrendForce；福邦投顧整理
Insertion 4為完整CPO模組測試，SLT方案仍待成熟：Insertion 4於32顆OE貼合至載板、與ASIC共同封裝後進行
，驗證完整CPO模組的功能與連通性，涵蓋Ins4e電性最終測試、Ins4o光學連通性（插入損耗）、Ins4eo光電同測
（加測回送誤碼率 ），以及在接近實際運作狀態下跑真實流量的 SLT。預計未來將加入光電同測 、SLT，目前
Ins4eo連測試參數都尚未完全確定 。測試難點在於CPO元件尺寸大、功耗高，FAU設計不統一，散熱頭與光學對
接存在空間衝突，且ASIC與OE需雙區溫控；SLT更需供應大量外部雷射光源，測試複雜度仍高。
台廠方面：FT純電、Ins4eo以Advantest/Teradyne搭配鴻勁分選機為主，Ins4o致茂(tester)。
圖20、CPO模組FT測試架構示意圖 圖21、ASIC+OE共封裝後測試重點整理

22【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
2028年測試設備TAM達400億元以上，Ins3、4占逾7成
資料來源：各公司、福邦投顧整理預估
TAM往後段集中 ，Ins3與Ins4為主要成長動能： 隨OE需求在2028年持續放大 ，測試設備 TAM明顯成長 ，其中
Insertion 3與Insertion 4的成長幅度皆高於整體，2028年合計占逾7成。Insertion 3須逐顆取放並全數檢測，是確保
Known Good OE的最後關卡；Insertion 4則須驗證整顆CPO封裝的光電功能，並逐步導入SLT，單顆測試價值最高
。若業界跳過Insertion 2，原本在晶圓階段的測試需求也將移往Insertion 3，進一步推升後段測試需求。
台廠以致茂、鴻勁為主要受惠者：致茂同時切入Insertion 3的OE測試與Insertion 4的光學測試及SLT；鴻勁以光電
同測Handler切入Insertion 4；穎崴、旺矽則分別提供測試座與晶粒探針台。

### Page 23

23【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
Ins2 不做可以節省成本，且隨 SoIC 製程良率提升測試時間減少
圖24、每產出一顆良品OE所需測試時間比較
資料來源：福邦投顧整理預估
SoIC 良率越高，不做 Ins2 每顆良品 OE 所需測試時間越少：不做 Ins 2 可省下每顆 die 在 Ins 2 的 45 秒，代價是
SoIC 不良品會額外占用 Ins 3 的測試時間，因此節省幅度取決於 SoIC 良率。良率約 60% 時兩者打平；良率越高
、不良品越少，每產出一顆良品 OE 所需的測試時間由良率 60% 時的 187.5 秒降至 80%/90% 時的 140.6/125.0 秒
，相較有做 Ins 2 分別節省約 28/38 秒（-17%/-23%）。
整體測試設備TAM縮減，但 Ins3 機台需求增加：若省略 Ins2，SoIC 後的良品與不良品 OE 都將直接進入 Ins3 受
測，受測量放大為 1÷SoIC 良率倍，Ins3 因受測 OE 增加，所需機台數也將大幅提升。但因省下整段 Ins2 機台，

整體而言 2027/2028 年測試設備新增 TAM 仍將分別減少約 12%/16%，可為客戶省下更多設備投資成本。

### Page 25

25【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
省略Ins2推升貼合機需求，2027年貼合機TAM增加約24%
圖26、FAU貼合位置示意圖
資料來源：TSMC、福邦投顧整理預估
FAU為光纖與PIC之間的耦合介面 ，現行有兩種貼合設計： FAU貼合於光引擎側邊 ，負責將光纖與PIC耦合
。現行設計分為兩種：先貼 receptacle再安裝FAU，以及直接將FAU貼合於OE上；前者可能需要兩道貼合製
程，後者僅需一次。
省略Ins2後不良品也須貼合 ，2027年貼合機需求增加約24%：以直接貼合FAU估算，有做Insertion 2時，僅
SoIC後的良品OE需要貼合；若省略Insertion 2，未經篩選的不良品也會一併進入貼合製程，使2027年貼合機
TAM增加約24%。隨SoIC良率提升、不良品減少，兩者差距逐步收斂 ，2028年已大致相當 。這也代表在量
產初期良率仍低時，省略Insertion 2的成本不只是測試，也會反映在貼合機台與貼合材料的額外消耗上。

26【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
Receptacle設計若需兩段貼合，2028年貼合機TAM有望達100億以上
資料來源： 萬潤、高明鐵、惠特；福邦投顧整理預估
Receptacle 設計有望使貼合機 TAM 最高倍增：以每次貼合約 3 分鐘估算，直接貼合 FAU 於 2027/2028 年新
增貼合機 TAM 約 43/51 億元；receptacle 設計須經 receptacle 貼合與 FAU 安裝兩段製程，若兩段皆需同等時間
的主動對位，TAM 最高可增至 85/101 億元。
台廠萬潤為主要受惠者，高明鐵、惠特送樣中：萬潤為目前最主要的 FAU 耦合貼合設備供應商，已出機至矽
品；若最終採 receptacle 兩段貼合設計，貼合機需求最高可倍增，萬潤作為現行主要供應商受惠幅度最大。其
他台廠方面，高明鐵、惠特目前均在送樣階段，國際競爭者則包括 MRSI 及港建代理的 ADST。

Earnings above all 
• Higher oil prices are in a tussle with stronger growth. We expect strong growth 
to help the global economy weather inflation risks, allowing equities to focus on the 
strength of earnings growth. Oil prices remain the key risk to monitor 
• Maintain a preference for equities over bonds and cash.  Strong earnings 
growth means we would use the recent pause to add exposure to our preferred 
markets and themes. The US and Asia ex-Japan remain our preferred regions 
• Take advantage of higher bond yields via credit risk but cap duration. The 
risk of higher bond yields looks most pressing in ultra -long maturities, but credit 
quality remains strong. We are Overweight EM USD bonds 
Hits from the 1990s 
Rising bond yields have been a key market theme.  Since mid -year, 10 -year 
government bond yields have risen approximately 40-80bps across the US, Japan and 
Euro area. Against this backdrop, gold has delivered high single -digit returns, while 
global equities have delivered low single-digit returns. 
Higher oil prices are in a tussle with robust growth data . The lack of progress 
towards a negotiated settlement in the US -Iran conflict and rising fears of shipping 
restrictions extending to the Bab al-Mandab Strait have meant that oil prices are once 
again testing the USD 100/bbl level. These worries have been exacerbated by 
increasing worries about refined product (eg. diesel, jet fuel, etc) supply. Despite these 
concerns, US growth data has held up remarkably well, as illustrated by the latest PMI 
and retail sales data. This is underpinned by AI investment growth.  
Real, rather than nominal, energy prices are key.  A chart of inflation -adjusted 
energy prices, though, illustrates why the global economy has managed to hold up 
despite these risks. Oil prices in real (inflation-adjusted) terms are closer to long term 
averages rather than at true ‘oil shock’ levels. This, together with the global economy’s 
lower oil intensity relative to history, is why we expect  growth and inflation to avoid 
worst-case scenarios as long as nominal oil prices avoid an excursion into the USD 
120-150/bbl range.

Global Market Outlook 4

Fig. 1 Our quant model score for equities has

The Fed is likely to hike twice more by mid -2027. The 
acceleration in AI-driven investment growth means we believe 
the chances of a shift from our core soft-landing to no-landing 
economic scenario have gone up. This, combined with still -
high inflation readings mean we now expect the Fed to hike 
rates twice more by mid -2027. In contrast, the BoJ and ECB 
are expected to hike thrice and once more respectively. 
Buy-the-dip in equities 
1990s rate hikes a better parallel then 2022 . There is 
understandably some concern about whether rising central 
bank policy rates, and rising bond yields across maturities, will 
present a challenge to equity markets. However, we believe 
rate hikes in the 1990s are a better historical parallel, when 
central banks were hiking rates into strong economic and 
earnings growth, resulting in equity markets maintaining their 
pace of gains. The 2022 hiking cycle, in contrast, represented 
an inflation surprise, which we believe is not the case today. 
Strong earnings growth overwhelms other factors today. 
Still-strong economic and earnings growth means that we 
remain constructive on the outlook for equities and other risky 
assets going into year -end and beyond. The pause over the 
last couple of months was likely driven by a combination or 
rising bond yields , September seasonality and excessive 
investor optimism around mid-year.  
However, earnings expectations for global equities remain 
very strong (especially in our preferred US and Asia ex-Japan 
regional markets). We believe these earnings remain 
sufficiently robust to outweigh the drag from higher bond 
yields. Therefore, w e remain Overweight equities relative to 
bonds and cash, though we believe it is prudent to ensure the 
size of the equities Overweight is not excessive. Within Asia, 
we also trim China equities to a core holding, leaving Taiwan 
as our only regional Overwei ght, given the ongoing focus on 
AI investment-driven markets for now. 
Bumps remain part of the journey. As we laid out in our mid-
year outlook, the second half of this year is expected to be 
characterised by positive, but bumpy returns as a result of the 
factors laid out in the previous section, but also US mid -term 
election risks. The second half has thus far played out as 
expected given sufficient bumps, but still robust earnings 
growth. We would not be surprised by some volatility around 
US mid-term elections, but any further pullbacks or pauses 
would be opportunit ies to raise exposure given strong 
earnings growth is expected to ultimately dominate. 
Prefer credit over duration 
Corporate credit quality remains strong . Aggregate 
indicators of credit quality suggest credit quality remains 
strong across both Investment Grade and High Yield bonds 
across most regions. US IG bond yields face some upward 
pressure from significant hyperscaler supply, but supply -
driven spread widening tends to be relatively short -lived in 
nature and should not signal significant credit quality worries. 
Longer-maturity bonds remain at risk of further volatility. 
In contrast, we continue to be concerned that the volatility in 
very long maturity bonds could extend. Continued inflation 
and debt worries mean it is possible investors continue to 
demand a greater ‘term premium’ (ie. a premium for holding 
bonds with very  long maturities). We continue to believe 
limiting bond duration to 3-7 years remains appropriate.  
We continue to prefer  corporate and EM over government 
bonds. This implies earning a higher yield by taking on more 
credit risk (via corporate or EM bonds) rather than adding 
more duration risk. Within bonds, we remain Overweight EM 
USD bonds and view IG and HY bonds as core holdings. 
Modest USD weakness; gold to grind higher 
USD bounce led by pricing of Fed hikes . However, we 
believe Fed hikes are now excessively priced. At the same 
time, we see room for non-US rates to rise further, especially 
in Japan where the undervalued JPY is likely to strengthen. 
This is why modest USD weakness is likely to resume. 
Gold to continue grinding higher. While the move higher is 
likely to be characteri sed by two -steps-forward-one-step-
back, we believe an ultimately softer USD and continued EM 
central bank demand should result in continued gains in the 
precious metal over both the next 3- and 12-month periods.

### Page 7

Global Market Outlook 7

Summary

With the Federal Reserve’s latest decision to raise its benchmark rate by 25 basis points in September 2026, as well as the one 
to two additional hikes predicted  before the end of the year, we are entering a new interest -rate hiking cycle. This marks an 
important shift for investors, but higher policy rates do not necessarily imply weaker portfolio returns. What matters is why rates 
are rising, how quickly they increase, and whether economic growth can absorb tighter financial conditions. 
Our framework places the current environment in a healthy/high-yield regime. Growth remains resilient, credit spreads are 
tight and the yield curve is normalising. At the same time, the economy is approaching an important boundary. Inflation remains 
above target and the 10-year US government bond yield has reached 5%. A renewed rise in inflation or a sharper slowdown in 
activity could move markets into an unhealthy/high-yield regime. 
This argues for  measured risk-taking rather than an aggressively defensive stance . We maintain a slight overweight in 
equities, hold a strong overweight in gold and a neutral allocation to fixed income. Diversification remains key near a regim e 
boundary, and hedge funds can provide useful sources of return that are less dependent on the direction of traditional markets.

Not all interest-rate hiking cycles are the same

Traditional market 
analysis often treats 
rising rates as uniformly 
negative. History 
suggests a more 
nuanced conclusion.  
We classify hiking 
cycles along two 
dimensions: whether  
the increase in rates is 
healthy or unhealthy, 
and whether it occurs  
in a high- or low-yield 
environment. 
 Fig. 8 The different regimes of interest-rate hiking cycle 
Historical interest-rate hiking cycle defined by real growth and US 10-Year treasury yields

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered; Since 1974

A healthy increase in rates is generally associated with firm 
demand, above -trend growth, resilient equities and stable 
credit markets. Central banks can move gradually and remain 
data-dependent because the economy is strong enough to 
withstand tighter fin ancial conditions. An unhealthy increase 
is more likely to be driven by supply constraints or persistent 
inflationary pressures. Growth slows, credit spreads widen, 
equities de-rate and central banks are forced to react more 
aggressively. 
The second dimension is the starting level of yields. In a high-
yield environment, bonds offer meaningful income and a 
greater cushion against moderate price declines. In a low -
yield environment, that protection is limited. This distinction 
matters because the same increase in yields can have very 
different portfolio consequences depending on the income 
available at the outset.

Assessing today’s market regime: Walking the 
fine line  
We assess the current environment as healthy/high yield 
(see Fig. 9), although it is moving closer to the boundary with 
an unhealthy/high-yield regime. Growth remains resilient, with 
2026 consensus GDP growth of around 2.1%, but it is below 
its longer-term trend. Inflation has eased , but remains above 
target, with headline CPI at 3.4% year -on-year in August 
2026, leaving the Fed focused on the balance between 
persistent price pressures and a potential growth slowdown. 
Market signals over the next 6-12 months remain broadly 
supportive. The 10-year US government bond yield is around 
5%, providing the highest bond carry since 2007, while the 
yield curve is re -steepening after its prolonged inversion.  
Equities remain positive, supported by AI -related earnings 
and soft-landing expectations, and high -yield credit spreads 
are near post-global-financial-crisis lows. 
Overall, resilient markets and benign credit conditions support 
the healthy classification. However, slower growth and above-
target inflation leave little room for disappointment. Further 
disinflation would help sustain the current regime, while sticky 
inflation, a rapid rise in yields or a sharp growth slowdown 
could push it into unhealthy territory.  
Higher rates, different outcomes 
Historical returns vary significantly across the four regimes 
(see Fig. 10), but history shows that some hiking regimes 
have generated solid returns across major asset classes. 
Unhealthy regimes are more complicated. Unhealthy/high -
yield periods (top-left corner) have historically still generated 
positive average returns, but these averages can conceal 
considerable volatility and dispersion. The most damaging 
combination has been unhealthy/low yield (as in 2022), when 
traditional 60/40 portfolios declined by an average of 15.8%. 
The lesson is that the economic cause of higher rates, and the 
level of yields, matter more than the label "rate hike" alone.

equity/40% bond portfolio during rate-hiking periods.
